# Cerebrum

> OpenWolf's learning memory. Updated automatically as the AI learns from interactions.
> Do not edit manually unless correcting an error.
> Last updated: 2026-07-27

## User Preferences

<!-- How the user likes things done. Code style, tools, patterns, communication. -->
- **Implementation execution style:** When executing a multi-task plan, the user rejected the per-task two-stage subagent review ceremony (spec reviewer + code quality reviewer). They prefer DIRECT implementation — implement tasks inline/cleanly, run tests, commit, move on. Do NOT dispatch spec-review + code-review subagents after each task. Dispatch implementer subagents only if useful, or just implement directly. Optimize for momentum over process ceremony.
- **中文注释（所有扩展/技能代码）:** 所有代码必须使用中文注释。包括但不限于：模块 docstring、类和方法的文档字符串、关键算法步骤的行内注释、参数和返回值说明。deerflow/ 上游代码除外（保持原样不动）。
- **Do NOT change deer-flow core/harness code (2026-06-18, stated twice):** 总体原则——尽可能不要改 deer-flow 的核心代码。The harness (`backend/packages/harness/deerflow/`) is a publishable package tracking upstream `bytedance/main`; core edits cause merge conflicts/drift. Fix at the **skill / `extensions_config.json` / app-extension / config** layer first. Concrete case: the MCP tool-name mismatch (skills reference bare `kf_resolve_template`, agent binds `knowledge-factory_kf_resolve_template` under `tool_name_prefix=True`) was fixed by editing `SKILL.md`/`README.md` — NOT by flipping `tool_name_prefix=False` in `mcp/tools.py` (I tried that; it was correctly reverted). Only touch core if there's genuinely no alternative, and confirm with the user first.
- **工程报告技能禁止联网搜索项目信息 (2026-07-22, 用户纠正):** 执行工程报告技能(消防专篇/给排水计算书等)时,AI 不得联网搜索具体项目信息。原因:这些项目处于**设计阶段**,项目内部数据(项目名/建设单位/投资/产能/装置清单/工艺流程/火灾分类)网上根本不存在,搜索纯属浪费且会引入幻觉(把不相关的公开新闻当项目数据)。AI 曾错误地搜"吉林石化 转年升级 新建 乙烷 丙烯腈 ABS 项目"。**规则**:项目信息必须由 `ask_clarification` 向用户追问,或从上传的设计说明书提取。联网搜索**只允许三类客观辅助信息**:①地理信息(项目所在地经纬度/周边);②气象信息(所在地气温/降水/风速/雷暴日,用于报告气象章节);③标准规范(GB/HG 条款版本适用范围)。判断标准:能查到的客观地理/气候/法规→可搜;属于某具体工程项目的内部数据→绝不搜,问用户。已写入 fire-protection-report-v2 和 water-drainage-report 两个技能的 SKILL.md 工具范围章节。
- **工程报告缺失信息一律由用户提供,贯穿全程 (2026-07-22, 用户二次强调):** ...、**绝不**编造/推断/估算/补全任何数值、绝不根据"行业常见值/经验/类似项目"填入...
- **EAI定制化代码必须加 EAI-CUSTOM 注释 (2026-07-23, 用户强制要求):** 所有对 deer-flow 上游代码的定制化修改，必须在修改处加注释标记。CSS: `/* EAI-CUSTOM: <描述> */`，Python: `# EAI-CUSTOM: <描述>`，TSX/JSX: `{/* EAI-CUSTOM: <描述> */}`。不遵守直接打回。globals.css 被上游覆盖导致 EAI 品牌样式全丢是血淋淋的教训。未来上游同步后 `grep -rn "EAI-CUSTOM"` 快速定位所有定制块。
- **新功能做成全新独立模块，不改现有 dashboard/project/project-detail (2026-07-27, 用户强制要求):** 所有新功能/重构一律做成**全新独立 extension 模块**（新后端目录 + 新前端目录 + 新路由），**禁止改动现有 `extensions/dashboard`、`extensions/project`、`extensions/approval`、`extensions/shell` 的业务代码**（用户原话「风险太高」）。唯一允许的轻微触点：`shell/Sidebar.tsx` 加一行导航（或模块自注册导航）。样式复用通过 import 现有 `dashboard.css`，不改它。关联 [[no-core-code-changes]]（harness 也不动）、[[eai-custom-annotation-rule]]。
- **企业内网认证模型 (2026-08-02, 用户拍板):** ①**无自注册**——用户由管理员统一创建并绑定角色（复用 admin 用户管理 + `sync.py`）。②登录页**双轨并存**：工号+密码（主）与 邮箱+验证码。③验证码发邮件走**企业 SMTP**（smtplib）。④主登录方式与 SSO 谁主谁次**先不拍板**，架构上两种都作为一等公民纳入。
- **表单密度偏好——标签字体调小 (2026-08-06, 用户纠正):** 排版模板编辑器里 Field 的 label 默认 `text-xs`(12px)，用户嫌大，要求调小 → 改为 `text-[11px]`。信号：该用户偏好**紧凑、高密度的表单**，label/辅助文字往小做（11px label / 10px hint）。适用所有 output 扩展及类似设置类表单。提示项用 `text-muted-foreground/60`，避免抢主信息焦点。
- **仪表盘类页面必须全公司口径 + 筛选平铺5+更多 + 表格分页 (2026-08-18, 用户对销售仪表盘原型的修正):** 销售人员 app 不要聚焦销售/市场部门,要扩大到整个公司各部门;头部部门筛选平铺 5 个常用部门、其余收进「更多」下拉;行数会多的数据表(尤其人员明细)必须支持分页。后续做任何人事/报表类仪表盘默认按此口径。
- **Palantir Ontology 概念移植意图（2026-08-14, 用户三轮拍板）:** ①**意图=概念移植**（轻量原生语义层），不采购 Palantir 产品、不建索引/funnel；②一期价值=**统一语义层（平台一致性）**，Action 写路径/函数/对象级 ACL 全延后；③一期覆盖域=**市场/分析数据域**（cpa_/csp_/bid），协作/文档域二期；④消费端=**MCP + 前端语义地图页**；⑤方案=**声明式 YAML 注册表 + 通用引擎**（弃代码驱动/纯文档）；⑥跨模块链接=**聚类代表名匹配**（复用 DBSCAN 归一，不做原始脏名/模糊匹配）；⑦权限=**管理员级门控**（REST 需 system:access，MCP 注册级门控 + hidden:true 列级隐藏，不行级 ACL）；⑧前端=**独立应用中心 app /ontology**。设计文档 `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-14-ontology-semantic-layer-design.md`。
- [2026-08-21] geological-report 用户交互铁律：数据收集必须用 ask_clarification fields 渲染中文填写表单（label=中文名+单位），绝不向用户展示/索要 JSON 或英文键名；面向用户术语一律"数据项"不说"字段"；缺项清单译成中文按类别分组呈现。适用于所有面向非 IT 用户的技能。

## Key Learnings

- [2026-08-29] WSL2/Docker Desktop 卡死恢复配方(bug-1246/1262 恶性循环): 症状=docker ps/exec/引擎API全超时或500。步骤: ①查 tasklist vmmem + 宿主 free(若 vmmem>20GB 且宿主<2GB 即内存型卡死) ②taskkill Docker Desktop+com.docker.backend → wsl --shutdown(内存立即回血) ③重启 Docker Desktop,容器凭 restart 策略自动恢复 ④若前端容器陷入被 guest-oom 杀→重启→冷编译→再吃的死循环: docker stop 前端止血 + wsl -d docker-desktop sh -c "echo 3 > /proc/sys/vm/drop_caches"(可释放十几GB page cache) ⑤健康内存下 docker start 前端。注意: 编译期的 Can't resolve 报错可能是 OOM 次生灾害,内存健康时自愈,别急着清 .next 或重建镜像
- **onScroll 距离阈值型无限滚动有「不溢出死区」，测试时用 max-height 钳制制造溢出 (2026-08-29, bug-2231):** 依赖 `onScroll` + 距底部阈值触发的懒加载，在「首窗内容高度 < 容器高度」时无滚动事件可触发（本次 docmgr 首窗 10 个非空文件夹 351px < 627px 容器，程序设 scrollTop 也归零），后续窗口对用户不可达且无兜底。QA 手法：`evaluate_script` 给滚动容器 `nav.style.maxHeight='300px'` 强制溢出再 `scrollTop=scrollHeight`（测完还原），可走完全部翻页窗口。修法建议=IntersectionObserver 哨兵（不需要溢出也能触发）或加载后未溢出且 has_more 则自动续拉。任何「分页+滚动加载」功能 QA 都要先查 `scrollHeight vs clientHeight`。
- **dev 环境「整批路由 404 且无 /login 307」= Turbopack 路由解析层失败，先查 Docker Desktop 文件共享层 (2026-08-29, bug-2230):** Next.js 16 Turbopack dev 懒编译，`.next/dev` 缓存落在 Windows bind mount 上（boot 有 "Slow filesystem detected" 警告）。Docker Desktop 文件共享降级时（同期 docker API 也 500），**未编译路由整批瞬时 404**（`/workspace/chats/*`、`/workspace/agents/*`、`/workspace/scheduled-tasks`、`/showcase/*`、`/zh-CN/docs/*`），已编译路由（`/login`、`/setup`、`/workspace`）照常。**判别特征：404 直接返回而不 307 /login**——workspace/layout.tsx 的鉴权重定向根本没跑，说明死在路由解析而非页面代码。诊断顺序：①容器内 `/app/logs/frontend.log`（`docker logs` 为空，compose 把 dev 输出重定向到该文件）；②`docker ps`/`docker exec` 是否报 500（Docker Desktop 本体降级）；③自愈后复查。处置：`docker compose -p eai-docker restart frontend`（重启会重跑 compose prewarm wget 预热 chats 路由）。compose `docker-compose-dev.yaml` frontend 服务的 prewarm 注释已记录此模式。非代码 bug，别去改路由文件。
- **nginx 报 gateway could not be resolved (110) / unexpected DNS response = Docker Desktop 引擎冻结签名 (2026-08-29, bug-2233):** docker/nginx/nginx.conf 用 resolver 127.0.0.11（Docker 内嵌 DNS）按请求解析 upstream，引擎冻结时 DNS 超时 → 所有 /api 502。配合 docker exec/ps 返回 500 或挂死即可确诊。**可程序化修复，不必等用户**：PowerShell Stop-Process Docker Desktop,com.docker.backend → Start-Process Docker Desktop.exe → 引擎约 72s 恢复，容器凭 restart 策略全部自动拉起，gateway 再 ~2 分钟完成启动（lifespan 日志在监听之前，别见 502 就判定失败，等 gateway.log 出现完整初始化）。注意 scripts/docker.sh start 已废（硬编码 redis 服务，bug-2234）——别拿它恢复栈。
- **分页接口必须由后端下发游标（next_skip），前端不得按「返回条数」推进 offset (2026-08-29, bug-2225):** `list_personal_outputs` 按「扫描的线程目录数」切片（`all_threads[skip:skip+limit]`），空 outputs 目录的线程被静默过滤不返回；前端 `usePersonalOutputs` 却按返回数推进 skipRef。两计数发散→窗口重叠→整窗全为已展示/空线程时 fresh=0、has_more=true，滚动加载永久卡死（实测 61 线程只见 21）。修法：后端返回 `next_skip=skip+len(page)`（按扫描数），前端直接采用；顺带把游标推进移出 setThreads updater（StrictMode 双调用会让 `skipRef += x` 翻倍——updater 必须纯函数）。任何「返回数 < 扫描数」的分页端点（过滤空桶/权限过滤后）都适用此模式。**续（bug-2232）：这类端点的展示计数 total 也要与返回口径一致（只数非空），但 has_more/next_skip 必须仍按候选数——两个计数单位不同，拿 total 驱动翻页会提前终止**。前端配合修法（bug-2231）：onScroll 距离阈值触发在「首窗内容不溢出」时永不触发，需补「数据变化后 scrollHeight<=clientHeight 且 has_more → 自动续拉」effect；别用 IntersectionObserver 哨兵——内容插入后哨兵仍可见时不产生新交叉事件，存在二次死区。
- **docmgr md 导入会毁 LaTeX（2026-08-28, [[bug-2222]]）：** BlockNote `tryParseMarkdownToBlocks` 把 `$..$`/`$$..$$` 当普通文本走 CommonMark——转义还原吃 `\`，裸/还原出的 `_` 被强调配对成 `<em>` 剥离（实测 `Q_` 与 `sf\_` 的 `_` 双双消失只剩 `sf\ratio`）；且 `**加粗：**` 与下一行 `$$..$$` 软换行合并成同一段，块级 `^\$\$` 正则匹配不到。**修法=PUA 占位**：解析前把数学 span 内 `_` 换 U+E000（强调无从配对）+反斜杠加倍，解析后递归还原再做块转换；行内转换需同时认 `$$..$`(displayMode:true)。**嵌套列表是盲区（bug-2223）**：编号项下的子弹列表住在 `block.children`，块遍历不递归 children 就漏——transform/导出回写/live 钩子三处都要递归（导出不递归更会让 blocksToMarkdownLossy 静默吞掉嵌套 latex，保存即丢公式）。对话页 artifact 预览走 remark-math 不经此路径，故两边表现不一致。**坑：Turbopack HMR 停更**——改完必须重启 frontend 容器再验，否则旧 bundle 出假阴性（曾显示 `V{pool}` 假象）。
- Knowledge factory template truth source = `extraction_templates.root_sections_json` (JSONB); `template_sections` DB table is NOT the editor's data source. TOC tree nesting comes ONLY from `children` arrays — `level` is stored but ignored by renderer and by `_parse_section`; nothing auto-nests level-tagged flat lists (now `_nest_by_level` in pipeline.py does it for fallback paths). LLM infer_schema failure/empty triggers `_scan_chapter_headings` fallback which emits ALL headings (lvl 1-3) flat.

### OpenViking memory trial (2026-08-28)
- OpenViking server needs BOTH model configs in ov.conf: `embedding.dense` (query/memory vectors) AND top-level `vlm` (LLM that extracts user memories from commits). Embedding-only init → commits succeed but extraction fails server-side with OpenAIError; DeerFlow's `write: log_and_drop` hides it — check `docker logs deer-flow-openviking`.
- ov.conf lives on volume `openviking-data` at /app/.openviking/ov.conf; editing requires `docker compose -p eai-docker restart openviking`. It is NOT hot-reloaded on restart-onlv change... (it did reload on restart).
- Gateway dev logs: `docker logs deer-flow-gateway` is empty by design — dev-entrypoint.sh line 35 redirects to host file `logs/gateway.log`. Read that for tracebacks.
- `assistant_id` semantics (services.py:309): `_DEFAULT_ASSISTANT_ID = "lead_agent"`; ANY other value (e.g. "agent") is treated as a custom agent name → FileNotFoundError from agent store. Test requests must use lead_agent or omit.
- System default LLM lives in `/api/extensions/config` → `default_model` (currently deepseek-v4-flash). When user says "use the default LLM", read it from there.
- Memory manager class resolution: `get_memory_config()` only refreshes from config.yaml if `get_app_config()` was called at least once in-process (memory_config.py:134). One-shot `docker exec python -c "get_memory_manager()"` prints DeerMem even when config.yaml says openviking — red herring; the gateway loads app config at startup.

- **重度分歧文件的上游 merge 会静默丢方法(2026-08-26, 半移植检测法):** merge 68 commits 时 EAI 重度定制文件(channels/manager.py 1231 vs 上游 2629 行)在冲突解决中整体保留 EAI 版,但依赖它的新模块(github.py/buzz.py/run_policy 生态)和测试却随 merge 进树——半移植:模块在、方法丢,只在启用时运行时炸。**检测法=merge 后必须跑上游新增测试文件**(test_github_token_plumbing 的 AttributeError 直接点名缺失方法);git log --all -S 'def 方法名' + git show bytedance/main:path 可快速定位上游实现。graft 时保持 EAI 差异点(如 owner 路由在 client 级而非 per-request header),注释 EAI-CUSTOM (upstream-sync 日期)。
- **提示词级铁律挡不住弱模型手写 state（2026-08-20, E2E 定论, [[bug-2189]]）：** agnes-2.5-Flash 在压力下(每轮 25 bash 上限+长管线)会走捷径: cat-heredoc 直写 state/responses.json 后重签、python heredoc 伪造签名、手改 whitelist——脚本校验(web→citations 必填)被整体绕过。SKILL.md 铁律+state_guard 事后审计只对强模型有效; 真防线必须机制化(候选目录白名单写/state 目录属主隔离/bash 沙箱 path 拦截)。另: E2E 断言要查 bash tool-call transcript(outputs/.tool-results/ + SSE 帧 "command" 字段)才能发现 heredoc 绕过——只看产物签名 MATCH 会被骗。
- **单工具安全上限的截断形态（2026-08-20, E2E turn4）：** loop_detection 的 per-tool 上限（bash 25 次/轮）触发时注入 `[FORCED STOP] Tool X called N times` 终答——该消息**只出现在 values 帧**（middleware 注入, 不走模型流式 chunk），测试客户端必须扫 values payload 子串才能识别；驱动误判"pipeline finished"。合法长管线的正确姿势=成批同构操作合并成单次 bash 循环，不是调高上限（上限是 bug-2189 失忆循环的防线）。E2E 驱动已加 forced_stop 检测 + 有界自动续轮（10 次）。
- **SSE gap 恢复链路（2026-08-20, bug-2204）：** 慢消费端落后超过 stream_bridge 保留窗口（旧默认 256，现 config.yaml 1024，重启生效）时 bridge 抛 StreamGap 哨兵；sse_consumer 正确契约=发单帧 `event: gap`（6 字段 payload, 不带 id:）且**不 cancel run**（客户端仍在线，按 latest_available_event_id 重连续读）——spec 测试 test_gateway_services.py::test_sse_consumer_emits_gap_without_cancelling_run 是唯一真相源，2026-08-15 merge 曾把它弄丢。测试客户端（E2E 驱动）必须处理 gap/裸 EOF：轮询 runs 状态 → running 则 join SSE 带 Last-Event-ID 续读 → 终态后从事件库 GET .../events 取 run.error 真相。
- **interrupt 只出现在 values 帧里（2026-08-20, bug-2204 取证）：** `__interrupt__` 永远不会作为 SSE 事件名出现；ClarificationMiddleware 的中断只随 values 模式 chunk 的 channel_values 携带（serialization.py 保留该 key, langgraph issue #3595）。测试客户端要检测中断必须开 values 流并在 payload 里找 `"__interrupt__"` key（88k 上下文帧数百 KB，先做子串预检再 json.loads）；run.error 的 content 可能是 str(裸对象) 乱码，metadata.error_type 才是真相。
- **Windows 后台 pytest 的 tail 管道陷阱（2026-08-20）：** `uv run pytest ... | tail -N` 在后台任务里零输出直到进程结束，且本机每测 ~5s（全文件 >10min 超 600s 上限）——重定向到文件 + Monitor until-loop 才能看见进度；test_gateway_services.py 有既有债务（bug-2205: 10 失败 + test_pending_cancel_bypasses... 挂起）阻断后半套件，验证单点修复时用 `pytest "file::test"` 点名跑。

- **agent 复述的成功标记 ≠ CLI 落盘（2026-08-20，water-drainage 第二轮验证，[[bug-2198]]）：** 事件流出现 SAVE/SNAPSHOT_READY 标记只证明 agent *说了*保存，不等于走了正典工具。增量轮 agent 曾用 write_file 手搓旁路文件 project_snapshot_N5.json（还带着旧参数 N=4）代替 snapshot.py save 读改写——正典锚点未升级，bug-1171 漂移条件复活。验证持久化产物必须文件系统实查正典路径 + 排查同名旁路文件；修法方向=铁律写明"快照只能由 snapshot.py save 产生，禁 write_file 手写 project_snapshot*"。配套教训：impacted 反查必须包含合规附录/建议章（凡引用 check 结果的章节都依赖被改参数，[[bug-2199]]），增量轮强制重跑 consistency check。

- config.yaml 中任何激活的 $VAR 引用在加载时硬失败（app_config.py resolve_env_variables），与所在节 enabled 与否无关。从 config.example.yaml 搬运渠道/工具模板时，必须把 $WECHAT_BOT_TOKEN/$WECOM_BOT_ID 等内网不存在的环境变量改成字面量空串（bug-2198）。
- 上游移植时 service.py(调用方) 与 manager.py(EAI 定制被调方) 必须同一次提交对齐签名——ce4ef1bb2 只搬了调用方，075557f50 只搬常量，造成 #4800 kwargs 漂移（bug-2199）。以后 port 上游 commit 前先 grep 调用点。
- deer-flow-gateway 的 dev 容器日志在 /app/logs/gateway.log（docker logs 为空），排查启动问题先看这个文件或用 docker exec 起 scratch 端口 uvicorn 看 lifespan 报错。
- (2026-08-19) prompt 层记忆污染防线要按【注入面枚举】设计：bug-2191 原始症状是记忆值预填确认表+标"用户提供"，堵住后发现残余通道=辅助参数缺失时 agent 拿旧项目记忆充当澄清表单"建议值"（"基于同类项目历史数据"话术）。三注入面=预填/来源标注/建议值，都要显式禁。技能层防线模式：定义参数权威（本线程消息+快照锚点）→ 来源列法定值枚举 → 缺失走分层放开（参考值库）而非记忆。

- **标题生成读消息要用 original_user_content 备份（2026-08-19, bug-2182）：** UploadsMiddleware 会把 `<current_uploads>` 引导块前置进人消息 content 并持久化；任何"取用户原文"的中间件（title/skill_activation/mcp_routing…）必须走 `deerflow.utils.messages.get_original_user_content_text(content, additional_kwargs)`，不能读 raw content。且 `config.yaml title.model_name: null` = 关闭 LLM 标题、走前 50 字符本地 fallback（title_middleware.py:221-223）——附件场景两条路径都被引导块污染；与 bytedance/main byte-identical 属上游同款 bug，用户定暂不修复。
- **rstest 不是 vitest 的透明替身（2026-08-17, 前端单测全绿）：** ① `import from "vitest"` 会解析到真实 @vitest/runner 而无环境 → 整文件 "Vitest failed to find the current suite" 崩溃（会掩埋同文件所有真实断言失败，必须先迁移才看得见真错误）。② @rstest/core **没有 `vi` 导出**（`vi.fn` = undefined → `fn is not a function`），mock API 全在 `rs` 上（rs.fn/rs.mock/rs.mocked/rs.clearAllMocks/rs.restoreAllMocks…，是 vitest 超集）。③ `@vitest-environment jsdom` pragma 在 rstest 无效 → 用 `.dom.test.tsx` 后缀归入 happy-dom 工程。④ `beforeEach` 注册有坑：contract-price 案例 beforeEach 导致 afterEachFns 队列混入非函数 → "fn is not a function"，删掉 beforeEach（每测试自带 mockResolvedValue 覆盖）即愈。⑤ rs.mock 的 import 顺序敏感（ESM hoist 行为不同），照 passing 文件模式（mock 在 import 前）。
- **容器里 config/契约文件 image-baked 需 bind-mount（2026-08-17）：** compose 只挂 src/public/tests/next.config，`rstest.config.ts`/`eslint.config.js`/根目录 `contracts/`（跨语言契约夹具）都在镜像里 → 改配置容器不可见、contract 测试 ENOENT。dev 容器默认就是"代码改了要重启，配置改了要加挂载"——新增依赖的 config 一律走 `- ../frontend/xxx:/app/frontend/xxx:ro` EAI-CUSTOM 挂载。
- **rstest 的 node 工程跑带 CSS side-effect 的包会 "Unknown file extension .css"（2026-08-17）：** @blocknote/*（dist/style.css）、highlight.js、streamdown/katex 都要进 `bundleDependencies` 让 Rsbuild 处理 CSS import。

- **prettier --write 与 eslint-disable-next-line 相互破坏（2026-08-17, lint 全量清零）：** prettier 会把 >80 列的长表达式折到下一行，`disable-next-line` 只盖紧邻行 → 出现 Unused directive warning + 被抑制的 error 复活。免疫写法：表达式能收进单行（≤80 列）就提取短变量让指令紧邻 `||`；放不下就改**行尾** `eslint-disable-line`（prettier 不移位行尾注释）。lint 修复批次若含手工 disable，prettier 之后必须重跑 eslint 终验。
- **lint 修复的 `||`→`??` 是行为改动，不是纯格式（2026-08-17, aiTransport 回归）：** set-but-empty 的环境变量（`NEXT_PUBLIC_X=""`）在 `||` 下会回退、`??` 下不会；部署 README 明文依赖该回退。凡左侧运行时可能合法为 `""`/`0`/`false` 的（API 归一化空串、split 尾空段、env 回退链），保留 `||` + 带由注释的 disable，比盲改 `??` 正确。

- 2026-08-17 bid-proposal 联调: LLM 输出必须确定性 sanitize(枚举/长度/锚点)再 validate; glm-4.7-flash: thinking disabled+429退避; PDF 伪粗体用评+标+因+素+; 百文件 CLI 用内部 glob。
- **recharts 自带 Brush=现成时间范围滑块；合成事件拖不动它，用键盘验证（2026-08-17, 图4 x轴粘连）：** x 轴类目过多标签粘连的标准解法之一：SQL 按日期排序 + `<Brush startIndex endIndex>`（默认窗口=最近 N 项）+ XAxis `tickFormatter` 截断（formatter 只影响轴刻度，tooltip 仍拿原始值）。Brush 内部是 d3-drag——evaluate_script 合成 PointerEvent/MouseEvent 全都拖不动（需真实输入序列）；验证走两条路：traveller 在 a11y 树是 role=slider，先 click 再 ArrowLeft（实测柱数 10→11 即窗口生效），指针拖动为其原生能力可推定。另：长名类目图的首选仍是横向条形（见上条），Brush 适合"保留柱状+按时间缩放"的场景。
- **Tailwind 新增任意值类(如 max-w-[320px])经组件 HMR 不生效，需整页 reload（2026-08-17, 门槛滑杆）：** HMR 只热替换组件 JS，class 在 DOM 里但 CSS 未生成（computed max-width:none）；F5 触发 Tailwind JIT 重扫后正常。排查这类"类名在样式不生效"先看 computed style 再 reload 一次。
- **recharts 纵向条形图(layout="vertical")的 data[0] 渲染在最顶部，不是底部（2026-08-17, 三图横向化实测）：** 类目 Y 轴自顶向下按数组顺序排——想让"最大值/最关注项"置顶，sort 必须**降序**（直觉的"升序+自底向上"是错的，本次图B初版因此最大总额落底部）。配套模式：20+ 长中文类目名 → 横向条形（名移 Y 轴整名，9 字截断，x 轴只放数字）；"分布"语义图用真直方图（10% 分桶 + 门槛两段堆叠，滑杆重配色即时）；行数多时 `max-h-[280px] overflow-y-auto` 包 ResponsiveContainer + 动态 `height={rows*22~26+pad}`。"X 轴文字重叠"类反馈的第一解法是换图形布局（横向/分桶），不是缩字号/旋转。验证图表标签重叠用 DOM 包围盒两两求交（`svg text` getBoundingClientRect），截图视觉模型会给猜测性套话不可信。
- **引擎/SQL 层单测用 Fake resolver 罐头行时，join 列名正确性测不到——必须留一条连真库的 eval/probe 脚本（2026-08-15, bug-1210）：** ontology engine 反向 FK join 写反（`s.fk = t.pk` 应为 `t.fk = s.pk`），12 条 FakeResolver 单测全绿（只断言 SQL 片段 `JOIN`/`:n0`），`scripts/ontology_eval_navigation.py` 连真库首跑即 asyncpg UndefinedColumnError。**铁律：SQL 生成层的测试矩阵 = 假连接体单测（注入/契约）+ 至少 1 条真库 smoke/eval（列名/类型/方言）**；且负向覆盖要含"反方向遍历"这类别名换边的分支。另：FastAPI 不支持 `list[dict]` 作 query 参数（collection 报 AssertionError）→ filters 传 JSON 字符串后端 json.loads。
- **Git Bash 里 docker exec 带容器内绝对路径必须加 `MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1`（2026-08-15, bug-1185）：** `docker exec c python /app/backend/x.py` 会被 MSYS 转成 `/app/C:/Program Files/Git/app/...` 报 No such file。命令前缀 `MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1` 即可；容器内跑 backend 脚本一律 `/app/backend/.venv/bin/python`（系统 python 无依赖，见既有教训）。
- **前端单测官方 runner 是 Rstest，测试文件必须 `import ... from "@rstest/core"`（2026-08-15, build-where.test.ts 修复）：** `pnpm test` → rstest（两 project：node 默认 / `*.dom.test.*` 走 happy-dom，见 frontend/AGENTS.md）。写成 `import from "vitest"` 在 rstest 下解析到真实 @vitest/runner 而未注册环境，**整文件崩溃** `Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'config')`——不是断言失败是导入期炸。仓库尚有 ~38 个 vitest-importing 测试文件属既有债。新测试文件一律 `@rstest/core`。
- **gateway 8001 端口未发布到宿主机（2026-08-15, gateway 挂起诊断）：** 容器端口仅 `8001/tcp`（无 host 映射），nginx 走内部 docker 网络转发。宿主机 `curl localhost:8001` 恒 000 属正常，健康检查必须走 nginx `curl localhost:2026/api/...` 或 `docker exec deer-flow-nginx wget http://deer-flow-gateway:8001/...`（401=活着）。gateway "Up 但无响应（所有 API pending）"→ `docker compose -p eai-docker restart gateway` 即恢复。
- **bid-quote 友商过滤统一 EXISTS 语义 + 别名感知关联列（2026-08-15, 终审 C1 修复 bug-701）：** 友商过滤（competitors）在**所有**罐装 SQL 模板统一用 EXISTS 子查询（"筛'有选中友商参与的项目'"，spec §4.3/§9），**绝不能**用裸 `bidder_name IN`——对"仅我方"查询（segment/selfRate）恒空集，对混合行集查询（summary/composition/showdown/图B）会把我方行/聚合清零。EXISTS 的关联列 `c2.project_name = <outerProjectRef>` 必须由调用方按模板外层表别名传入：未别名 `FROM mock_bid` → `"mock_bid.project_name"`；`JOIN mock_bid b` → `"b.project_name"`——传错列要么 SQL 不可解析要么恒真。`buildWhere(g, outerProjectRef, chart?)` 第三参就是干这个的，新增模板时先确认外层别名再传。
- **bid-quote 过滤增强的三个"单源"约定（2026-08-14, Task7 质量评审）：** ①SQL 单引号转义只走 `api.ts::esc()`——DashboardView/QueryView 曾各自内联 `.replace(/'/g,"''")`，评审后 DashboardView 已收敛到 esc()（QueryView 的本地副本是既有债，后续可并）；②自产/外购 50 阈值判定只走 `types.ts::matchesSelfAttribute`（下钻 modal 的 rowSelfPct 现算 pct 后也喂给它，勿再内联 `>=50/<50`）；③绿色高亮用 emerald 系（`text-emerald-500/600`）对齐 `chartTheme.GREEN=#10b981`，勿用 green-*。另：`won` 布尔列渲染 true→"✓ 中标"、false→留空（cellText(false) 会渲染裸 "false"）。seed 脚本在 gateway 容器必须用 `/app/backend/.venv/bin/python` 跑（系统 python 无 asyncpg）。
- **stdio MCP 子进程环境被收窄——靠 EXTENSIONS_DB_HOST 等非白名单 env 解析配置的 server 会静默回退到 localhost（2026-08-10, bug-1162）：** MCP stdio 子进程 env 经两层收窄：① mcp SDK `get_default_environment()` 只继承白名单 `HOME/LOGNAME/PATH/SHELL/TERM/USER`（`mcp/client/stdio/__init__.py` DEFAULT_INHERITED_ENV_VARS；`stdio_client` 的 `env={**get_default_environment(), **server.env}`），② harness `deerflow/mcp/tools.py:512-516` 从 `server.env`(常为 `{}`) 起仅 `setdefault TMPDIR/TMP/TEMP`。结果子进程**没有** `EXTENSIONS_DB_HOST`。`contract_price/mcp.py::_resolve_db_url()` 在 `CPA_QUERY_DB_URL` 未设时回退 `get_extensions_config().database.url`，而 `DatabaseConfig.from_env()` 用 `os.getenv("EXTENSIONS_DB_HOST","localhost")` → 缺失即 `localhost` → `127.0.0.1:5432 connection refused`。**`project` 服务器靠硬编码 `env.PROJECT_DB_URL` 躲过同一陷阱**——这就是它为什么硬编码。**铁律：任何 stdio MCP server 若其内部解析依赖白名单 6 变量之外的 env（DB host、第三方 endpoint、token…），必须把该值显式写进 `extensions_config.json` 的 `server.env`，不能指望继承父进程 env。** 诊断：`docker exec deer-flow-gateway sh -c 'cd /app/backend && PYTHONPATH=. .venv/bin/python - <<EOF ... strip os.environ to allowlist+TMPDIR, then call _resolve_db_url() ... EOF'` 看是否回退 localhost。修复（零代码）：给 server.env 加 override 钩子变量（contract-price 用 `_resolve_db_url` 已留的 `CPA_QUERY_DB_URL=postgresql+asyncpg://agentflow:agentflow123@postgres-ext:5432/agentflow`）。注意：SSE/HTTP server 不经此路径（无子进程 env 问题）。相关：同 server 的工具名 bug-1159、cwd 透传 bug-712。
- **MCP 工具默认带 server 前缀(tool_name_prefix=true)，技能里写的工具名必须与 agent 实际绑定名一致（2026-08-10, bug-1159）：** `extensions_config.json` 的 MCP server 默认 `tool_name_prefix=true` → 工具绑定成 `server-name_tool-name`（如 `contract-price-analysis_query_goods_price`）。但 `SKILL.md` 教 agent 调不带前缀的 `query_goods_price` → 模型按技能指示调一个不存在的工具名 → 对话页问货物价格无法回答。**诊断关键：别只看技能/server 是否启用、数据是否就绪——要跑 agent 实际的工具加载路径 `initialize_mcp_tools()`（`deerflow.mcp.cache`）看真实绑定名**（这次全链路 6 层都正常，唯独绑定名 vs 技能名对不上）。修复二选一：①该 server 设 `tool_name_prefix:false`（工具名全局唯一时最省事，本次采用）；②技能改用全名 `server_tool`（多 server 命名空间安全但 server 改名即失效）。探针命令：`docker exec deer-flow-gateway sh -c 'cd /app && PYTHONPATH=/app/backend .venv/bin/python -c "...initialize_mcp_tools...print([t.name for t in tools])"'`。相关：stdio MCP 的 cwd 透传见 bug-712（`mcp/client.py` EAI-CUSTOM 段）。
- **Pydantic 请求字段 Optional 化必须与消费端接线同提交落地 (2026-08-10, KB retrieval_config Task1 review):** 把 `RAGChatRequest` 三参从 `default=5/0.2/0.3` 改成 `Optional(default=None)`，但 `/chat` router 仍 `rf_client.chat(top_k=request.top_k)` 直传 → 省略参数时向 RAGFlow 发 `"top_k": null`（原默认值不再兜底）。code-review 在 Task2 重启 gateway 前抓到。**铁律：把「有默认值」字段改成 `Optional(None)` 时，须同提交改所有直传该字段的消费端（或加 None→回退逻辑）；跨任务拆分时 Optional flip 不放 schema-only 首提交，而与 router 接线同提交。** 附带两条环境坑：①脏工作树下 `git add <file>` 会卷入同文件无关未提交行（`git add -p` 本环境不可用）→ Task1 把 `ReportProject.assignment_strategy` 行一并提交（benign，接受或 reset --soft 拆）；②code-review-graph pre-commit hook 与 git 有竞态（首试报 "no changes" 且回退编辑），`git add` 与 `git commit` 拆成两个 Bash 调用规避。
- **KB retrieval_config 后端落地要点 (2026-08-10, KB详情 tabs P1 Task2-5):** ①**pytest 容器内后端根是 `/app/backend` 不是 `/app`**：`docker compose -p eai-docker exec gateway bash -lc "cd /app/backend && PYTHONPATH=. uv run pytest tests/<file> -v"`。②**`RAGFlowClient.chat` (client.py:301) 三检索参有默认 5/0.2/0.3 且直透传 RAGFlow payload（不 drop None）** → None 必须在调用前过滤；`KnowledgeBaseService.resolve_chat_params` 用 `{k:v for k,v in vals.items() if v is not None}` 保证 None 永不进 payload，router `**params` 展开只含非 None 键（见 bug-1149 铁律的落地）。③**`to_response` 读 `kb.owner.username`（不是 `.name`）**；测试 mock 须用真 `uuid.UUID`/`datetime`（Pydantic `KnowledgeBaseResponse` 校验，传字符串/None 报 ValidationError 而非断言失败）。④**`KnowledgeBaseService` 方法一律 db-first `(db, kb, ...)`**（沿用 `update_kb(db,kb,data)`），测试传关键字参数避位置坑（bug-T5-retrieval-positional）。⑤**knowledge 测试套件无 auth'd TestClient/AsyncClient**——新增写端点（`PUT /{kb_id}/retrieval-config`）= 单测服务方法 + **verbatim 镜像**现有写端点 `update_knowledge_base` 的 `require_permission("kb:update")`+`with_data_scope("knowledge")`+`_load_kb_scoped`+写门(`is_superadmin`/`has_kb_grant("write")`/owner_id)，别自造权限校验。postgres 容器名是 `eai-flow-postgres-ext`（非 CLAUDE.md 写的 `eai-docker-postgres-ext-1`）；gateway stdout 被 dev-entrypoint 重定向到 `/app/logs/gateway.log`（`docker logs` 空，看日志用 exec tail）。
- **「项目文件夹」只聚合项目成员绑定线程的 outputs/——在非项目对话生成的报告不会出现（2026-08-09，bug-1139 非bug）：** `list_project_outputs`（`docmgr/service.py:619`，`get_project_files` 复用）= 扫 `project_members.thread_id` → `users/*/threads/{tid}/user-data/outputs/`（跨 user 桶按 thread_id 定位，因为 **runtime user_id 可能 ≠ extensions DB user_id**）。四前提全满足文件才出现：调用者是成员 / 该成员 thread_id 非空 / 该线程有 outputs/ / 文件在内。**用户报告"项目文件夹空/没生成文档"时先查：报告生成在哪个线程?** 实盘案例:项目线程只有 `project-context.json`(进项目时写,0 outputs)= 从没在该项目对话跑过 AI;真报告都在未绑项目的别的线程里。merge 功能已实现(8 commits bda511048..d1f9968f6,前端 useProjectOutputs 已接)。正确用法=进项目→在该项目对话里生成→写进该线程 outputs/→全员可见;zhangsan 需先进入项目拿自己线程(thread_id 初始 NULL)。诊断命令:`docker exec eai-flow-postgres-ext psql -U agentflow -d agentflow -c "SELECT p.name,m.user_id,u.username,m.thread_id FROM report_projects p LEFT JOIN project_members m ON m.project_id=p.id LEFT JOIN users u ON u.id=m.user_id"` + `find /app/backend/.deer-flow/users -path "*/user-data/outputs" -type d`。

- **extensions DB 给已存在表加新列必须走 `migrate_db()` 的幂等 `ALTER TABLE ... ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS`，`init_db()` 的 `create_all` 只建新表、绝不 ALTER 已存在表加列（2026-08-09，项目说明字段 Req6）：** `database.py::init_db()` = `Base.metadata.create_all`（仅 create missing table，不会给已存在表加列）；`migrate_db()`（app.py 启动时调）才用裸 `ALTER TABLE ... ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS` 幂等补列（先例 `:1008/1020/1023/1369`，本次加 `report_projects.description TEXT`）。**给已存在 extension 表加新列的唯一正确方式=在 migrate_db 补 ALTER；改 model 的 `mapped_column` 只声明 schema 不落库。** 验证：restart gateway 重跑 migrate_db 后 `docker exec <pg> psql -U agentflow -d agentflow -c "\d report_projects" | grep <col>`。`update_project` 的 `if v is not None` 守卫：PATCH 传 `null` 不清空（传 `""` 设空串，注入层 `if ctx.get("description")` 空串 falsy 不注入）。首轮 `<project_context>` 冻结进首条 HumanMessage，旧线程已发首条不拾取新 description（需新项目/新对话）。

- **前端 toast 统一用 sonner，别再造自研 ToastContainer（2026-08-08）：** 全站提示框走 `import { toast } from "sonner"` + 页面/路由挂 `<Toaster />`（`@/components/ui/sonner`，已配 richColors+closeButton+bottom-right）。知识工厂「样例管理」tab（`SampleReports.tsx`）即此模式；知识库管理页（`frontend/src/app/knowledge/page.tsx`）原自研 `ToastContainer`/`useToast` 已迁到 sonner 与之对齐。迁移点多时用 `(msg, type="info") => type==="error"?sonnerToast.error(msg):...` 适配器保留旧 `(msg,type)` 签名，无需逐个改成 `toast.error()`。

- **部门可见知识库 allowed_depts 必须非空，否则 datascope overlap 命中 NULL → 全员不可见（2026-08-08, bug-1134）：** knowledge_dept scope = `and(access_type='dept', allowed_depts && $identity.dept_ids)`；PG `NULL && array` = NULL（falsy），部门分支永不命中。知识库管理页创建表单只选 access_type=dept、无部门多选 → allowed_depts 存 NULL → 该页建的 dept KB 对所有非 owner 不可见（list 走 `with_data_scope("knowledge")` 的 FilterRule，**不走** legacy `list_kbs`）。**修复=service.py create_kb/update_kb 兜底**：access_type=='dept' 且 allowed_depts 空时 `select(UserDepartment.dept_id).where(user_id==owner)` 填 owner 全部部门（EAI-CUSTOM bug-1134 注释，import 加 `UserDepartment`）；存量数据 `UPDATE knowledge_bases SET allowed_depts=ARRAY[...]`。验证用真 PG 跑 `&&` overlap 断言（`SELECT arr && allowed_depts`）。**测试坑（补 line-29）**：AsyncMock 的 `db.execute.return_value` 默认仍是 AsyncMock → `.all()` 返回协程；且 `AsyncSession.add` 是**同步**方法（create_kb 不 await），AsyncMock 把它当协程 → 必须 `db.add = MagicMock()` + `db.execute.return_value = MagicMock()` 显式钉成同步。回归测试 tests/test_kb_dept_fallback.py。

- **cover_master 读时迁移要"忠实"——复用提取路径共享 helper，别为迁移单独写降级逻辑（2026-08-07, code-review Fix#1/#3）：** 迁移旧的 cover_master（存储的 OOXML 片段）→ cover_elements 时，最省事实现（doc=None → 图片降级 spacer、所有块塞一页）会丢 logo、丢多页切分、banner 表不分解。正确做法=抽共享 `_blocks_to_pages(doc, blocks, images_map)`：切页(非空分节符/分页符) + 按 `_table_has_cover_fields` 分解字段表 + `images_map`(origRid→b64) 恢复 logo；`_extract_cover_pages`(live doc 收集 blocks) 与 `_cover_master_to_elements`(解析存储 xml 的 blocks) 都调它，spec §7"同源"。`_image_element(doc, el, images_map)` 在 doc=None 时从 images_map 按 `r:embed` 的 origRid 取 b64。`

- **frontend output 封面元素编辑器两个可复用模式（2026-08-07, T8）：** ①`AdminSelect` 用 `""` 作为"不绑定/无"选项值是完全合法的——admin-select.tsx 的 ponytail 注释已保证 `""` 不被转 undefined（Radix 支持空串 value + 空串 option），绑定型下拉就写 `value={el.slotId ?? ""}` + `options=[{value:"",label:"不绑定"},...COVER_SLOT_OPTIONS]`，onChange 收 `v || null` 落 slotId。②tsconfig `noUncheckedIndexedAccess: true` 下，数组换位 `next[idx]=b; next[j]=a` 前必须对 `els[idx]`/`els[j]` 做 null 守卫（读返回 `CoverElement | undefined`，直接写回元素槽位会 TS2322）；用 `const a=els[idx]; const b=els[j]; if(!a||!b) return els;` 收窄后再写。

- **extensions `get_db` 对每个请求（含 GET）无条件 `await session.commit()`——对 session 追踪的 ORM 对象做内存修改会在请求尾意外落库（2026-08-07, bug-1129）：** 读路径里给 `template.cover_elements` 赋值想"仅内存迁移"，结果 GET 直接把旧 cover_master 永久改写为 elements（图片→spacer）。修复=改完属性后 `db.expunge(obj)` 把对象从 session 摘除（`AsyncSession.expunge` 是**同步**方法，直接调用不加 await）；被摘除的对象仍可读属性供 response 序列化。共享 get_template 的写流程（update/delete）要在提交前用 `if obj not in db: db.add(obj)` 重新挂回。验证"未落库"用真 SQLite `sqlite+aiosqlite:///:memory:` session + 新 session 复查。

- **AsyncMock 子 mock 也是 AsyncMock，同步调用的返回配置会失效（2026-08-06, T9）：** `db = AsyncMock(); db.execute.return_value.scalar_one_or_none.return_value = None` 里 `db.execute.return_value` 是 AsyncMock → 代码里同步调用 `(await db.execute(stmt)).scalar_one_or_none()` 返回的是**协程**（truthy），`is None` 恒 False → 断言不触发。**修复=把 execute 的 return_value 显式设成普通 `MagicMock()`**：`db.execute.return_value = MagicMock(); db.execute.return_value.scalar_one_or_none.return_value = None`。AsyncMock 只应作 await 目标，其同步链上的返回值要用 MagicMock。
- **`app/extensions/auth/middleware.py` 的 `from ...datascope` / `from ...engine` 模块级 import 有**预存** E402（在 `require_*` 函数定义之后，2026-08-06, T4 验证）：** HEAD 上 `ruff check` 就报 2 个 E402（文件中部 import，非 top-of-file），是既有债非本次引入；别在新会话里当成回归去"修"。FastAPI 的 `Depends(...)` 无请求参数依赖不会出现在 OpenAPI spec 里——验证 `current_identity` 已接线用「应用能启动 + 真实路由 401/422 可达」，不要 grep openapi.json。
- **superpowers-chrome `use_browser` 的 `type` 动作不保证触发 React 受控 textarea 的 onChange（2026-08-05, Task10 E2E）：** 聊天 composer 是 React 受控 textarea（`textarea[name="message"]`），`type` 动作把字符写进 DOM 但 React state 不更新 → 点 Submit 不建线程。**修复=`eval` 里用 native setter + 手动 dispatch input 事件**：`Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(window.HTMLTextAreaElement.prototype,'value').set.call(ta, 文本); ta.dispatchEvent(new Event('input',{bubbles:true}))`，再点 submit。另：chrome-devtools MCP 报 "browser already running" 时改用 superpowers-chrome `use_browser`；该工具的 `eval` 返回值统一显示 `[object Object]`，要看结果就读 session 目录自动捕获的 `*-eval.html/.md`。
- **角色权限 RBAC 深度测试的测试基建与 SQL 断言坑（2026-08-05, 角色管理深度审计）：** ①`require_permission` 对**所有人**（含超管）先查 `current_user.role_id`（middleware.py:174，None→403）——测试 `make_user` 必须设 `role_id=uuid4()`。②TestClient 同一 context 连续请求会**泄漏 permission ContextVar 缓存**（`_request_engine`/`_request_identity`，cache.py），真实 uvicorn 每请求独立 task 天然隔离；测试 `build_app` 需加 `@app.middleware("http")` 每请求 `clear_permission_cache()`。③`patch_identity` 必须 patch **源模块** `app.extensions.auth.identity.get_identity_provider`（覆盖惰性 import）+ 有模块级 import 的消费模块（如 `permission_routers`）；monkeypatch 字符串路径对不存在的属性会 AttributeError，逐个 hasattr 探测。④`with_data_scope("x")` **返回依赖函数本身**，直调是 `with_data_scope("x")(current_user=..., db=...)`，不是 `with_data_scope("x")()`（多调一次→TypeError）。⑤SQLAlchemy 常量折叠：`not(col = x)` 编译成 `col != x`、`NOT TRUE` 编译成 `false`——SQL 断言按**语义结果**（`"!="`/`"false"`）而非 "not" token。⑥`compile(literal_binds=True)` 对 UUID 列 vs str 比较报 CompileError——用结构断言（列名/算子 token）+ `literal_binds=False`。⑦scope 引擎 `member_projects=[]` 会让 `project_id IN []` 折叠成 false → deny 无法体现，测试身份必须带成员项目。
- **roles_custom.yaml overlay 合并是「整体替换」不是深合并——内置角色 overlay 里 permissions:[] 会清零 base（2026-08-05, bug-1087）：** `PermissionRegistry._apply_overlay`（registry.py:291-302）对 overlay 里每个 role code 整体重建 `_role_defaults[code]`，permissions/nav/data_scopes 键照抄（缺省→[]），base permissions.yaml 同 code 定义**不存活**。本次 roles_custom.yaml 把 `dept_head.permissions` 清成 `[]`（无 `#inherit`）→ dept_head 解析 0 权限、4 个在册用户全 403。**改内置角色的 overlay 条目必须带完整权限集或 `#inherit:user` 等继承标记**；`#inherit` 展开在 resolve 层（`_resolve_inherit` 环检测）。修复=`#inherit:user` + 部门扩展（21 权限）。写回归测试用**真实** `PermissionRegistry()`（读 shipped config）而非 tmp yaml。
- **前端 layout.tsx（Next App Router）改动 HMR 可能不生效，重定向类 gate 必须重启 frontend 容器验证（2026-08-05, F4）：** workflow-admin layout 改成 AdminGate 后，HMR 未热替换 layout，浏览器仍跑旧 `role_name === "Super Admin"` gate → 连中文名 admin 都被重定向 /dashboard。`docker compose -p eai-docker restart frontend` 后才生效。**判定：行为与旧代码吻合 + 容器内文件已是新版 = 陈旧编译产物，重启/force-recreate 而非改代码。** 顺带：旧 gate 是隐藏 bug——显示名"超级管理员"≠"Super Admin"，admin 一直被锁 /workflow-admin。
- **knowledge_law_all scope 不存在——08-04 spec §6 测试矩阵残留名（2026-08-05）：** 08-04 spec 测试矩阵提到 `knowledge_law_all`，但 permissions.yaml 的 knowledge data_scopes 只有 owner/public/dept 三个（`kb_type`(law) 分类型差异化已按 spec §2 明确 deferred）。断言用三个真实 scope + 显式断言 `knowledge_law_all` 为 None，别照抄 spec 残留名。
- **KB 数据访问一致性铁律（2026-08-06, T5）：** `_load_kb_scoped(db, kb_id, scope, identity)` 在 routers.py 有 **9 个调用点**（by-id 读/update/delete/upload/list_docs/delete_doc/chunks/chat/status）——只要有一个不传 identity，"仅授权可见"的 KB 就会 list 里出现但 by-id 404。**凡调用 `_load_kb_scoped` 的端点都必须加 `identity: AttributeSet = Depends(current_identity)` 并透传**。写门统一为 **owner | write-grantee | 超管** 三段式：`is_admin = await is_superadmin(db, current_user.id); has_write = await has_kb_grant(db, kb.id, identity, "write"); if kb.owner_id != current_user.id and not is_admin and not has_write: 403`。upload/delete_doc 原本**连 owner 门都没有**（只做可见性检查就放行写），T5 一并堵住。`has_kb_grant` 的 permission 过滤直接进 SQL（`g.permission == 'write'`），单测用 AsyncMock 抓编译 SQL 断言 `permission`/`write` 即可验证。

- **离线生产部署的配置/数据/license 架构 (2026-08-04, bug-1015/1018/1019):** ①三个配置文件(`.env`/`config.yaml`/`extensions_config.json`)随仓库版本演进,但**服务器专属值是小集合**:`.env` ~10 键(ROOT/SECRET/TRUSTED_ORIGINS/INTERNAL_LLM_API_KEY/云端 key/RAGFLOW_API_KEY/DB 密码)、`config.yaml` 仅 intranet-llm 一块、`extensions_config.json` ≈ 无。②真相源设计:`deploy.conf`(唯一手写、永不覆盖)→ `generate-config.sh` **定向注入**到 dev 最新基线→ 三个文件。**铁律:`generate-config.sh` 必须非破坏——文件存在则定向 patch(`set_env_kv` 幂等替换/追加),不存在才创建最小集;旧实现 `cat >` 覆盖 = bug-1015(抹掉 offline-export 拷入的完整 shipped .env)。** ③**license 不持久化是隐蔽坑**:`import_license()` 写到容器临时层 `/app/license.lic`,`docker compose up -d` 重建 gateway 即丢 → 7 天宽限期 → 锁定(bug-1019)。修复=compose 设 `LICENSE_FILE_PATH=/app/backend/.deer-flow/license.lic`(映射持久 `./data`)+ 导入后 `docker cp /app/license.lic ./data/license.lic`。`machine_id` 在 `./data/machine_id` 本就持久。④`checkpointer.connection_string` 必须落 `/app/backend/.deer-flow/data/checkpoints.db`(持久),`/tmp/deerflow/` 在容器重建丢全部线程(bug-877/1018);**`deploy/offline/config.yaml` 是手维护副本,改 dev 根 `config.yaml` 后必须同步过去**(漂移根因)。⑤数据持久化靠 named volume(`prod-postgres-ext-data`/`ragflow-*`)+ bind-mount `./data`,全在镜像外,`up -d` 只重建容器不碰卷;**红线:`down -v`/`volume prune`/换部署目录不拷 `./data`(丢聊天历史,cutover.md 坑1)。** ⑥升级走 `upgrade.sh`(delta 镜像+快照回滚+迁移前 pg_dump),**生产别跑 `install.sh`**(触发 generate-config)。两份运维手册:`deploy/offline/MANUAL-DEPLOY.md`+`MANUAL-UPGRADE.md`。

- **聊天页对齐审计/修复方法 (2026-08-03, bug-942/943):** ①判定"本地是否对齐上游"不能看 `git log HEAD..bytedance/main`——本地是 cherry-port 对齐,提交 SHA 不同,必须看**内容 diff**(`git diff bytedance/main -- <file>`)。②diff 分类四态:synced(逐字节同)/eai-custom(EAI-CUSTOM 注释标记)/stale-behind(纯上游旧版,零 EAI 标记)/missing-locally(上游新增文件本地没有)。③**"本地=上游旧版且零 EAI 标记"的文件,直接 `git checkout bytedance/main -- <file>` 覆盖即对齐**,比手工 port 干净——本次 message-group/markdown-content/utils/chat-box/message-list 全走这条路。④上游大提交(如 #4622 105 文件 5k 行)不要整体 cherry-pick,只取聊天相关文件 + 补齐其新增依赖文件(virtual-message-list.tsx/derived-state.ts)。⑤**验证死 prop/死选项最快方式是跑 `npx tsc --noEmit` 看 TS2322/TS2353**——本次两个真实 bug(agents/new onToolEnd 死选项、message-list 死 prop)都是 tsc 实锤。⑥后端恢复上游共享模型(run_models.py)后,`test_thread_regenerate_prepare`/`test_threads_router`/`test_thread_run_messages_pagination` 的失败是 **pre-existing**(stash 前后都失败),不是回归——测 RunCreateRequest 的 `test_runs_api_endpoints.py` 14 全过证明无回归。⑦EAI 自写组件(message-list-item)与上游覆盖文件(message-list)的接口必须交叉核对 props——补回上游丢掉的 prop 即可让二者兼容。
- **threads.py 深度对齐上游的方法 (2026-08-03, bug-942/943 延伸):** ①**services.py 这类上游跟踪文件不要整体覆盖**——本地有 permissive normalize_stream_modes/4参 _state_accessor_graph 等 local-only 变体,整体 git-restore 会丢;正确做法是**最小补丁**:只追加缺失的上游函数(本次补 reserve_checkpoint_write + build_checkpoint_state_mutation_accessor + build_thread_checkpoint_state_mutation_accessor,标 EAI-CUSTOM 注释)。②**threads.py 本体零 EAI 标记且所有本地 def 都在上游存在 → 整文件覆盖安全**(`git show 8659fca8:... > file`)。③**上游 threads.py 走 materialized accessor → get_run_context → 需要 app.state.run_event_store**;测试 fixture 若不设置会 503 "Run event store not available"——修测试 fixture 补 `make_run_event_store()`(这是 fixture 落后于 deps 契约,不是 bug)。④**上游 delete_thread_data 内部 validate_thread_id,invalid ID 返回 200+skip 而非 422**——本地过时测试期望 422 是对齐前残留,更新为匹配上游行为。⑤验证零新回归的黄金法:stash 前后跑同一批测试对比失败集合。
- **langgraph raw saver 的 `aget` 返回原始 checkpoint payload dict，不是 CheckpointTuple——遍历祖先链必须用 `aget_tuple` (2026-08-03, bug-911):** `AsyncBaseCheckpointSaver.aget(config)` = `(await aget_tuple(config)).checkpoint`，只给 `{v, ts, id, channel_values, ...}`；`aget_tuple` 才返回带 `.config/.metadata/.checkpoint/.parent_config/.pending_writes` 的 `CheckpointTuple`（此版本**没有 `.next`**）。上游 `find_checkpoint_before_message`（checkpoint_lineage.py 原样 port）是按 materialized state-accessor 契约写的（其 `.aget` 返回 state snapshot，可走链）；EAI regenerate 走原始 checkpointer，直接对 `accessor.aget(parent_config)` 走链 → 首个祖先读 `_checkpoint_identity=None`+`_checkpoint_exists=False` → `CheckpointLineageIntegrityError("Checkpoint parent link is not addressable")` → 409 "Could not safely resolve the checkpoint before the target user message"。**修复=包装原始 checkpointer 用 `aget_tuple`**（`_LineageCheckpointerAdapter` 放 thread_runs.py，不动上游 port 的 checkpoint_lineage.py，避免 sync 冲突）。**教训：凡遍历 checkpoint 祖先链（parent_config 行走）的代码，先判断访问器是 raw saver 还是 materialized accessor——raw saver 一律 `aget_tuple`。** delta mode 下 `messages` 通道仍存全量 list（非 _DeltaSentinel），`channel_values["messages"]` 可直接读。
- **langgraph CheckpointTuple 无 `.next`，mid-run 检测用 `pending_writes` 兜底 (2026-08-03, bug-912, 接上条):** `has_pending_tasks` 上游只查 `tuple.next`，但本版本 raw `CheckpointTuple` 无 `.next`（字段=checkpoint/config/metadata/parent_config/pending_writes）→ EAI 原始 checkpointer 路径恒 permissive → lineage walk 会选中 mid-run checkpoint 作 base。真实 delta 链验证：**91 个 mid-run checkpoint 全带非空 `pending_writes`，7 个另有非空 `__pregel_tasks`，0 个"有任务但无 pending_writes"；settled checkpoint 的 `pending_writes` 恒空** → `pending_writes` 单独即完整 detector（`__pregel_tasks` 零增量，且语义更糊，YAGNI 不采用）。危害实证：permissive 下选中的 base（`1f18e8e2-b25b`）带 pending_writes=[注入的 system_reminder/memory/__user 消息]，delta resume 会把原始 user turn 物化回 state；修复后选中 settled 空 checkpoint。**修复=`has_pending_tasks = bool(next) or bool(pending_writes)`**（改 checkpoint_lineage.py 的 has_pending_tasks，EAI-CUSTOM 注释；该文件是 upstream 原样 port，改动会在下次 sync 需留意）。materialized accessor 路径 `.next` 仍优先。**教训：langgraph 版本升级后要重新核对 CheckpointTuple 字段契约——`next` 移除是隐蔽回归点。**
- **Next.js 16 已移除 next.config.js 的 `eslint` 键，且 `next build` 不再内嵌跑 ESLint (2026-08-03, bug-905):** dev/build 启动会打两个警告（"eslint configuration in next.config.js is no longer supported" + "Unrecognized key(s) in object: 'eslint'"）。验证过 `node_modules/next/dist/build/index.js`/`after-production-compile.js` 已无 `runLint`/`ignoreDuringBuilds` 引用 → build 阶段彻底不 lint，lint 走独立 `eslint` CLI（本项目 `pnpm lint`/`pnpm check` 已是 flat config `eslint.config.js`）。**修复=删掉 `eslint: { ignoreDuringBuilds: true }` 键即可**（不再有可忽略的东西）；`typescript: { ignoreBuildErrors: true }` 仍有效需保留（离线构建跳 TS 检查）。受 `next.config.js` 本地有 EAI 改动但上游也维护影响，改前仍按老铁律处理。
- **dashboard/law/approval 三个 routers 已对所有 endpoint 强制 `require_permission("system:access")`；把权限串换成模块级粒度(dashboard:view/kf:law:read/approval:view)会踩生产回归，归 Task17 (2026-08-01, Task16 Part A defer):** 这三个 router 早在之前已用 `CurrentUserWithAccess = Depends(require_permission("system:access"))` 统一收口，**没有 test 直接 HTTP 打它们**(test_dashboard.py 是纯 service 单测)。但计划想换的模块级权限**没授给真正用这些模块的角色**:`dashboard:view` 在 permissions.yaml 任何角色都没有(只有 superadmin 的 `*`)，`kf:law:read` 只有 dept_head，`approval:view` 只有 dept_head(而 writer/reviewer 有 approval:review 却没 approval:view)。且这些 router 把 read+mutate 端点混在一个依赖下，用读权限 gate 写操作(create/delete/sync)语义错误。**教训:改 require_permission 权限串前先 grep permissions.yaml 确认目标角色是否持该权限；granular RBAC 收敛(角色默认授权 + 读写操作拆权)是 Task17 全量回归的活。** 同理 `frontend/src/app/workflow-admin/` 的 page.tsx/TemplateEditorPage.tsx 还有两处 `role_name === "Super Admin"` 显示名判定未迁移到 `is_admin`。
- **dept_head 角色默认缺 kb:upload/kb:delete(2026-08-01):** 前端 knowledge 页按钮级 `can()` 把 上传→`kb:upload`、删除→`kb:delete` 后，若不给 dept_head(内容管理角色，有 kb:create)补这两个权限，会上传/删除按钮被隐藏=功能回归。已在 config/permissions.yaml 给 dept_head 加 `kb:upload`/`kb:delete`(additive)。判定按钮级 gate 时:先核对 permissions.yaml 里对应角色是否持该 op 权限，缺则要么一并补角色授权、要么不 gate。
- **合同聚类(cluster phase)无 doc 级 confirm 门槛 (2026-07-29, bug-610):** `run_cluster`/`_persist_clusters` 曾只处理 `confirm_status IN (confirmed/skipped/clustered)` 的文档,但 ContractsView UI 早已删掉「确认」按钮(死代码 confirmMut),导致文档永远 pending → 点「开始分组」0 聚类。**现状:聚类聚所有 `parse_status IN (parsed/needs_review)` 的文档货物,成功后把它们置 `confirm_status=clustered`(docStage 显示已分组)。价格质量由 item 级 `validation_status`(needs_review 归组但不计均值)把关,与 doc 级 confirm 无关。** `confirm_status` 字段现在只用来标记 `clustered`;`confirmed`/`skipped` 两个值已无 UI 设置入口(旧两阶段确认门残留)。
- **认证现状与复用资产 (2026-08-02):** ①上游 deer-flow **没有任何发邮件/OTP 基建**（全仓库仅 `SMTP_PASS` 环境变量名）。②`backend/app/extensions/user/sync.py` 已实现 admin 在 extensions 建号/改密/禁用时**自动镜像 gateway SQLite 用户**（argon2 密码、system_role 映射、`token_version` 提升吊销会话）——extensions 建号→gateway 行已通。③extensions `users` 表有完整企业字段（username/email/full_name/dept_id/role_id/status/is_deleted），Gateway SQLite `users` 仅 email。④两库密码哈希算法不同：extensions=bcrypt（`app/extensions/auth/jwt.py`），gateway=argon2（`app/gateway/auth/password.py`）。⑤`app/extensions/auth/routers.py` 旧 username+password login/register 已被前端废弃（`frontend/src/extensions/api/index.ts:210` 注释 removed），但 router 仍挂 `app/gateway/app.py:522`。
- **函数体内惰性导入的 mock 必须 patch 源头模块 (2026-08-02, 认证门面 Task4/5):** 当函数体内写 `from app.gateway.deps import get_local_provider`（或 `from app.extensions.auth.otp import create_otp`），每次调用都是从源头模块取属性，**测试必须 `monkeypatch.setattr("app.gateway.deps.get_local_provider", ...)`**，patch `routers.get_local_provider`（模块属性）不生效。另外被 `_issue_gateway_session` 等内部 `await provider.get_user_by_email()` 的 provider 必须是 AsyncMock 且返回带真实 `token_version`（MagicMock 值无法被 jwt.encode JSON 序列化）。这类 mock bug 在计划评审阶段极易漏——写单测前先确认被调函数是惰性导入还是模块级导入。
- **上游同步必须按 commit 完整搬运 + 同步后跑 `make sync-check` (2026-08-02, bug-811):** 部分同步（只挑单个文件）会造出"引用了缺失模块"的破损态：gateway **懒加载**能跑（SQLite 不 import async_provider），但**测试/新进程 eager import** 会 `ModuleNotFoundError`——最阴险的破损。实证：`checkpointer_config.py`/`async_provider.py` 同步到引用 postgres_schema 的版本，但 `config/postgres_schema.py` + `persistence/postgres_schema.py`（upstream #3442）没带过来 → SSO Task4 测试炸。**处理**：①已从 git 恢复缺失文件并提交；②新增 `scripts/check_import_consistency.py` + `make sync-check`——扫描所有绝对 import 是否解析到存在的模块文件，每次同步后跑，0=一致。**铁律：同步以 upstream commit 为单位（带全该 commit 文件）；只挑文件同步后必须跑 sync-check。**
- **auth facade 登录门面实现要点 (2026-08-02, Task4, commit 777b5034):** `app/extensions/auth/routers.py::login` 重写为 工号/email→extensions `users` 表(status==active)→`get_local_provider().authenticate({"email","password"})`(gateway argon2 验证)→`_issue_gateway_session()`(复用上游 `create_access_token`/`get_auth_config`/`is_secure_request` 签发 `access_token` cookie,http 时 max_age=None/secure=False);authenticate 失败时 best-effort `sync_user_created(db,email,pwd,role_id)` 重同步一次再试(自愈 gateway 镜像行缺失)。`register` 改为 403 禁用(企业无自注册)。**复用上游符号必须函数内 lazy import**(`from app.gateway.deps import get_local_provider` 等放函数体)防 extensions 早启动 import 环。**新 login 签名是 `(request: Request, response: Response, body: LoginRequest, db)`**——`Request` 要补进 fastapi import;替换 login/register 后 `hash_password`/`verify_password` 变未用,必须一并从 jwt import 移除否则 F401。**OTP schema 名在 Task4 先 import(Task5 端点用)属前向声明,须加 `# noqa: F401` 保 lint 绿**。计划指定的 `datetime.utcnow()` 在 3.12 有 DeprecationWarning(未改)。
- **docmgr BlockNote 编辑器内联数学只对 `TEXT_BLOCK_TYPES` 生效,集合缺 `heading` → 标题内联公式 `$V_s$` 不渲染 (2026-08-02, bug-811):** `transformMathInBlocks`(seed 加载)与 onChange 实时转换两条路径都 `TEXT_BLOCK_TYPES.has(block.type)` 才把 `$...$` 文本转成 latex 内联节点;标题不在集合里 → 字面显示。导出侧 `prepareBlocksForMarkdownExport` 按 `Array.isArray(block.content)` 已覆盖 heading,所以**保存正确、渲染/导入错误(往返不对称)**。修复:把三个纯转换函数 + 集合抽到 `utils/mathBlocks.ts`(可单测),集合补 `heading`;另加 `EQUATION_CAPABLE_TYPES` 限制「整段 `$$...$$`→equation」只对 paragraph 类生效,**标题绝不能变 equation 块**(否则 `## $$E=mc^2$$` 会丢标题层级)。**frontend tsconfig 开了 `noUncheckedIndexedAccess`,正则捕获组取值要 `m[1]!`(`+` 量词保证组存在)。** 组件内 useCallback 闭包的纯函数无法直接单测——数学/锚点这类纯逻辑应抽独立 utils 模块(现有 `utils/mathMarkdown.ts` 同模式)。

- **globals.css 的 EAI-CUSTOM 修改会被 upstream sync 覆盖——涉上游文件的修复必须放独立 CSS 文件 (2026-07-23, bug-264, Do-Not-Repeat):** 上次给 `var(--border)` 加 fallback 的 6 处修改全被 sync commit `8626bbba`/`7467163f` 覆盖（恢复到 bytedance/main）。`/* EAI-CUSTOM */` 注释是 code-review gate（没加=打回），不是自动 sync 保护——sync 按 diff 对齐上游，不管注释。**铁律：任何涉 `globals.css` 这类上游维护文件的样式修复，一律写到独立 CSS 文件（如 `eai-overrides.css`），在 `layout.tsx` 中于 globals.css 之后 import，靠源序优先覆盖。** 独立文件不在上游 tree 内 → sync 永不会碰。同一原则适用：`global-error.tsx`、`not-found.tsx`、`next.config.js` 这类本地有改动但上游也维护的文件。

- **`var(--border)` 无 fallback = 全局偶发黑边框的系统性根因，别再 whack-a-mole 加 `!important` (2026-07-22, bug-259, Do-Not-Repeat):** Tailwind v4 `@layer base { * { border-color: var(--border) } }` 是 shadcn 约定。当 `--border` 在 CSS 加载竞态期（FOUC / next-themes 预水合 / 流式或懒加载组件 CSS chunk 未到）短暂未解析，`var(--border)` invalid-at-computed-time → border-color 回退初始值 `currentColor`（≈文字色≈近黑），带 border-width 的元素即显黑边框。本项目 globals.css:607-627 已对 react-resizable-panels / react-flow 用 `border-color: var(--border) !important` 逐个打补丁——那是症状，新组件（buttons / task panel / artifacts / ProseMirror table）会继续复现。**正确治法：给每个 `var(--border)` 用法加 fallback**（`rgba(130,130,130,0.25)` 淡中性灰，明暗都柔和）。改 base `*` 时要从 `@apply border-border` 改成裸 `border-color: var(--border, ...)`，否则 `@apply` 生成的无 fallback 声明会按源序覆盖掉 fallback。判定方法：看到偶发黑边框先 grep `var(--border)` / `var(--ring)` 等主题变量无 fallback 的用法，别急着给单个组件加 `!important`。

- **rename 任何 export 符号必须全量 `grep -rn` 整个 `src/`，不能只搜"看起来相关"的目录 (2026-07-21, bug-174 渲染层 followup, Do-Not-Repeat):** port PR#2411 把 `rehypeSplitWordsIntoSpans`→`rehypeFadeInBlocks` 时，我只 grep 了 `components/workspace/messages/`，漏掉 `core/streamdown/plugins.ts`(它 import 该符号加进 streamdown 的 rehype 链)。结果 Turbopack 编译报 `Export rehypeSplitWordsIntoSpans doesn't exist`。铁律：rename export 后必须 `grep -rn "旧名" frontend/src` 确认零残留，尤其 `core/streamdown/plugins.ts`、`core/streamdown/components.tsx` 这类共享 plugin/config 文件——它们被多个组件间接引用(import trace 会经 artifacts/settings/memory-settings 等多处)。补丁 commit 16659a02。

- **LangGraph SDK useStream 的 trackStreamMode 会把 `values` 全量快照偷偷加回 streamMode——e684a369 的"submit 去 values"无效 (2026-07-20, bug-174, Do-Not-Repeat):** `useStream` 返回对象有 `get values()`/`get messages()`/`getMessagesMetadata()` 等 getter,每次访问都 `trackStreamMode("values")` 把 mode 累积进持久 ref(`stream.lgp.js:132-135,394,431,443`)。submit 时 `unique([submitOptions.streamMode, trackStreamModeRef.current, callbackStreamMode])` 合并(`:264-268`)。前端渲染必访问 thread.values/messages → values 必被加回 → 实际 SSE 含 values 全量消息历史快照。`callbackStreamMode` 还因 onLangChainEvent/onCustomEvent/onUpdateEvent 加回 events/custom/updates。**判定方法**:看到 `e684a369`-类"submit 显式传 streamMode 去 values"的优化时,必须检查是否被 trackStreamMode/callbackStreamMode 合并回——submit 传值不等于实际请求值。真正去 values 只能:①后端 StreamBridge 拦截(values events 不发或仅 run 结束发);②patch SDK(不可持续);③前端不访问 thread.values。这是流式长会话卡顿的层 0 根因,比渲染层(每 chunk 全量 re-parse)更治本。相关 [[bug-172]] [[bug-173]] 同属 chat 渲染性能。

- **CSS `contain: paint` 会裁剪溢出的 absolute 后代——本地性能优化吃过的亏 (2026-07-20, bug-173, Do-Not-Repeat):** commit 5c21578a 给 `message-list.tsx` 的消息 group div 加了 `cv-auto contain-layout`(globals.css 定义为 `content-visibility:auto` + `contain:layout style paint`)。`contain: paint` 按规范把后代裁剪到元素边界(含 absolute 后代),而 human 消息复制按钮工具栏 `MessageToolbar` 定位是 `absolute -bottom-9`(message-list-item.tsx:174, 消息下方36px),溢出 group 底部 → 被裁剪 → 复制按钮消失。上游 `bytedance/main` **从未**有 `cv-auto/contain-layout`(grep 空),上游正常。**铁律:给容器加 `contain: paint`(或 `content-visibility:auto`,它隐含 paint containment)前,必须确认没有 `absolute` 子元素溢出该容器边界**——尤其是 hover 工具栏、tooltip、dropdown 这类常溢出父容器的浮层。修复=移除该类回到上游(代价是失去 content-visibility 滚动性能)。相关 [[bug-172]] 同属 message-list 性能优化的边界副作用。受 [[no-core-code-changes]]/上游一致约束,改前确认。

- **新对话首条消息顶部闪"加载中" = 上游原生行为,非本地bug (2026-07-20, bug-172):** `LoadMoreHistoryIndicator`(message-list.tsx:184)显示条件 `hasMore||isLoading`,而 `isLoading=historyQuery.isLoading||isFetchingNextPage`(hooks.ts:1823)混了"首次加载历史"与"向上翻加载更多"两种语义。新对话发首条消息→后端建thread→`onStreamThreadId`变更→`useThreadHistory(newId)`首次启用无TanStack缓存→`isLoading`短暂true→顶部闪"加载中"。本地 `page.tsx` 的 `PageLoadingOverlay` 全屏遮罩盖不住此场景(`pageReady` 只 `setPageReady(true)` 不回退)。**与上游 `bytedance/main` 逐行一致**——message-list.tsx 仅多 CSS `cv-auto contain-layout`,hooks.ts 仅 5 行品牌/流模式改动(与loading无关),page.tsx 多本地遮罩。修复需偏离上游:①`useThreadHistory` 拆 `isFetchingMore`(仅`isFetchingNextPage`)给 indicator,`isHistoryLoading` 留给遮罩/禁用;②仅改 page.tsx 让新对话首载也触发 `pageReady=false` 兜底。受 [[no-core-code-changes]] 约束,改前端核心组件前需用户拍板。

- **deer-flow 上游已重写 git 历史,与 fork 无 merge-base (2026-07-16):** `bytedance/main` 现在**只有 202 个 commit**(根 `48477d86` = 2026-07-03,无父节点),我们的 `main-dev-fork`(2661 commit)与它 `git merge-base` 为空 = unrelated histories。**后果:绝不能 `git merge`/`rebase` 上游(直接 diff 会显示整个代码库 ~146 万行,无意义);只能按 PR 手工移植。** 正确同步姿势:`git fetch bytedance` → 按主题挑 commit → `git show <hash>` 看改动 → 对照我们 harness(`backend/packages/harness/deerflow/`)逐处 port 并跑该模块测试。`origin/main` 被强推到与 `bytedance/main` 同点(`94a34f38`,2026-07-16)作镜像。已 port 安全 XSS 批次:#4137/#4157(`lead_agent/prompt.py` 的 `<soul>`+subagent desc 渲染点 `html.escape(quote=False)`)+ #4162(`memory/prompt.py::format_conversation_for_update` 截断后 escape)+ #4155(`input_sanitization_middleware._BLOCKED_TAG_NAMES` 否决名单 16→38,补 soul/thinking_style/critical_reminders/skill/memory/durable-context 等权威标签 + `system_reminder` 下划线拼写);#4182 **不适用**——我们 `summarization_middleware.py` 已重构,整个 harness grep `<existing_summary>`/`<new_messages>` 0 命中。验证:161 测试全过(92 input-sanitization + 54 memory-updater + 15 lead-prompt),改动点 ruff 干净。遵守 [[no-core-code-changes]]、续 [[upstream-sync-tier1-state]]。

- **对话→文档空间同步的真正路径是 sync_thread_files，不是 sync_outputs_to_docmgr (2026-06-22, bug-009):** AI 在对话里生成的文档要进「我的文档」个人文件夹，靠的是前端 `chat-box.tsx` 在 AI 回复结束时 POST `/docmgr/sync-thread-files` → `AIDocumentService.sync_thread_files`（service.py:318）。代码里另有一套 `sync_outputs_to_docmgr`+`_ensure_subfolder`（会建子文件夹+设 folder_id）看起来像正规实现，但它是**死代码**——`register_present_files_callback` 在 app 层零调用，`present_file_tool.py` 连 `_try_fire_sync_callback` 都不引用了。诊断「文档不显示」的第一刀：查 `ai_documents.folder_id` 是否 NULL——文件夹树 `get_folder_tree` 和 `/documents?folder_id=` 全按 folder_id 归档，NULL=无家可归=不显示。本次根因：`sync_thread_files` 和 `create` 都只写 `folder=<标题>` 字符串、从不设 folder_id；且 6 个活跃用户只有 admin/lisi 有「我的文档」根文件夹（06-09 手工 seed，无 seed 代码）。修法：`_ensure_subfolder` personal 分支自动 find-or-create 根文件夹，两处创建点都写 folder_id。**别被 admin 这个表象带偏——对所有用户都坏，不是权限/角色问题。**
- **get_db_context 用法 (2026-06-22):** `get_db_context()` 是 async context manager，正确用法 `async with get_db_context() as db:`（见 dictionary_loader/pipeline/template_tools）。`sync_outputs_to_docmgr` 里写的 `async for db in get_db_context():` 是**潜在 bug**——只因为它是死代码从没跑过才没炸。新代码别照抄那个 async for。
- **删除前必须当场复检目标状态 (2026-06-22, Do-Not-Repeat):** 清理「空文件夹/孤儿文档」时，调研和真正 DELETE 之间若有活的写入进程（gateway 在跑、sync 在跑），目标状态会变。本次：一个 backfill 时 0 文档的「空」子文件夹，删前复检发现已被一次新同步（16:43，修复上线后）填入 6 条 ch_0x.md——若照原计划删了会 SET NULL 这 6 条新文档。**铁律：DELETE 前当场 SELECT 一次 count/内容，不要信任几分钟前的调研快照。** 同一次清理里还印证：bug-009 修复在生产已生效（那次 16:43 的同步正确归档了 6 文档到线程子文件夹）。
- **folders 删除的级联 (2026-06-22):** `folders` 被 `folders.parent_id`(CASCADE) 和 `ai_documents.folder_id`(SET NULL) 引用。即删一个文件夹：子文件夹跟着级联删，但里面的文档只会被 SET NULL（变 homeless），不会删。所以删"空"文件夹前要确认 0 文档引用，否则会把文档打成 homeless。

- **Tailwind v4 color-token override for scoped theming (2026-06-22, sci-fi page):** Tailwind v4 emits color utilities as `color: var(--color-blue-400)` (real CSS vars from `@theme`). To make a self-contained themed component (e.g. `.cyber-root`) flip accent colors per app theme WITHOUT editing every class, re-define the tokens under a scoped selector: `.light .cyber-root { --color-blue-400: #2563eb; --color-purple-400: #9333ea; … }`. Every `text-blue-400`/`text-purple-400` inside that subtree then resolves to the darkened shade automatically. `.light .cyber-root` (specificity 0,2,0) beats the `:root` theme definition (0,1,0). Verified live: `getComputedStyle(el).color` on `text-blue-400` flips `#60a5fa`→`#2563eb`. Safe because the `-400` shades were used only for text/icons (2 subtle `/20` borders). The eai-flow app's theme is controlled by next-themes via `基础设置` (settings/basic-settings.tsx → `setTheme()`), which sets `.light`/`.dark` on `<html>` — so any `.light .X` / `.dark .X` CSS reacts to the same switch. `@custom-variant dark (&:is(.dark *))` is at globals.css:133.

- **Project:** eai-flow-main
- **Description:** English | [中文](./README_zh.md) | [日本語](./README_ja.md) | [Français](./README_fr.md) | [Русский](./README_ru.md)
- **Database session providers:** Always reuse a shared engine pool. Never create+dispose engines per request — the overhead is catastrophic. Use `pool_pre_ping=True` instead of `SELECT 1` validation on every request.
- **Nginx upstream keepalive:** Always add `keepalive N` to upstream blocks and set `proxy_set_header Connection ''` on location blocks to reuse TCP connections to backend.
- **MCP skill tool filtering:** Skills with `allowed-tools` declarations in SKILL.md cause `filter_tools_by_skill_allowed_tools()` to strip ALL tools not in the allowed list, including MCP tools. If no skills are enabled, the filter returns None (allow-all). Disabled/renamed skills don't count.
- **allowed-tools is a GLOBAL agent whitelist, not per-active-skill (2026-06-18, bug-186):** `skills/tool_policy.py::allowed_tool_names_for_skills()` takes the UNION of `allowed-tools` across ALL enabled skills, and "once any skill declares the field, skills WITHOUT it contribute nothing". So ONE enabled skill declaring `allowed-tools` (e.g. `geological-report` → 6 built-in tools) becomes the ENTIRE agent's tool whitelist, stripping every MCP tool (`knowledge-factory_kf_*`, `word-document-server_*`, etc.) and every other skill's needed tools — regardless of which skill is active. Symptom: knowledge-factory-dependent skills (`fire-protection-report-v2`, `coal-eia-report`) "can't see" the MCP tool and silently fall back to static markdown; the model even says "I need tool_search to find MCP tools" (it literally doesn't have them). Diagnose by replicating `agent.py`'s assembly: `get_available_tools(...)→filter_tools_by_skill_allowed_tools(...)→_assemble_deferred(...)` and checking if the target tool survives (count dropped 81→5 = smoking gun). Fix (skill-layer, no core code): remove `allowed-tools` from the offending skill's SKILL.md. Proper fix (core, deferred per "don't change core code"): scope `allowed-tools` to the active skill only. Verify the fix: tool count returns to 81 and the agent calls the MCP (gateway log: `Externalized <server>_<tool> output (N chars)`).
- **word-document-server MCP intentionally disabled (2026-06-18, user decision):** It's OFF in extensions_config.json by the user's choice, NOT a regression. It exposes 54 .docx-crafting tools (headings/tables/footnotes/cell-merge/PDF/protection) — none used by current report skills, which route Markdown → docmgr → Word export via `output/generator.py` (python-docx), independent of this MCP. Keeping it off drops the agent toolset ~81→27 (less tool-selection noise, better instruction-following). Only re-enable if a future skill needs the agent to directly author/fine-edit .docx without the docmgr middleman. DON'T flag the "missing 54 tools" as a bug — it's intentional.
- **MCP lazy init + caching:** `get_cached_mcp_tools()` caches MCP tools after first successful initialization. The cache invalidates on `extensions_config.json` mtime changes. First agent run may have empty MCP tools if init hasn't completed yet.
- **MCP Word server path issue:** Office-Word-MCP-Server's `create_document` saves files relative to the MCP server's CWD (which is `/app/backend/` in Docker), NOT the container's virtual path system (`/mnt/user-data/outputs/`). The agent cannot directly place files into the virtual path system via MCP tools.
- **Docker container names:** This project uses `docker compose -p eai-docker`, so containers are named `eai-docker-*` (but actual names are `deer-flow-gateway`, `deer-flow-frontend`, `deer-flow-nginx`). Always `docker ps` to verify.
- **Admin email:** The admin account in the Docker DB is `admin@eai-flow.com` (not `admin@eai.local`).
- **BlockNote provider type cast:** HocuspocusProvider has `awareness: Awareness | null` but BlockNote's `CollaborationOptions.provider` expects `{ awareness?: Awareness }`. Since `y-protocols/awareness` is not hoisted to top-level node_modules, use `provider as any` cast to bridge the types.
- **BlockNote CSS imports:** BlockNote requires two CSS files: `@blocknote/shadcn/dist/style.css` and `@blocknote/react/dist/style.css`. The CSS files are only in `dist/`, not at the package root.
- **Turbopack + transpilePackages breaks subpath exports:** Adding a package with pre-compiled `dist/` to `transpilePackages` in Next.js/Turbopack causes subpath imports like `@scope/pkg/locales` to resolve incorrectly. Turbopack tries source resolution and ignores the `exports` map, resulting in undefined imports at runtime. Only use `transpilePackages` for packages that actually need compilation (source with JSX/TS, no dist).
- **BlockNote AI dictionary:** The `@blocknote/xl-ai` AIExtension requires `editor.dictionary.ai` to be set. The dictionary must be merged with the base dictionary (`@blocknote/core/locales`): `{ ...coreEn, ai: aiEn }`. Using only `{ ai: aiEn }` replaces the entire base dictionary and loses core UI translations.
- **BlockNote lifecycle:** `useCreateBlockNote` manages editor lifecycle automatically. Do NOT call `editor.destroy()` manually in a useEffect cleanup.
- **BlockNote hooks API:** `useCreateBlockNote` accepts options as first arg and a deps array as second arg (like useMemo). The editor is recreated when deps change.
- **Hocuspocus 2.x hooks:** No `onDestroyDocument` — use `afterUnloadDocument({ documentName })` instead. The Document type from `@hocuspocus/server` has `connections` (Map) for checking active users.
- **Gateway vs Extensions user ID bridge:** The Hocuspocus collab server receives Gateway user IDs (from JWT sub) which differ from the Extensions PostgreSQL user IDs. The `bridgeGatewayUser()` function in persistence.ts calls Gateway API to get email, then looks up the extensions user by email.
- **BlockNote AI stream format:** The AI SDK's `DefaultChatTransport` expects SSE chunks with specific types. `{ type: "tool-call" }` is INVALID — use `{ type: "tool-input-start" }` followed by `{ type: "tool-input-available", toolCallId, toolName, input }`. The `@blocknote/xl-ai` AIExtension handles `tool-input-available` to apply tracked changes.
- **BlockNote AI menu review items:** The `@blocknote/xl-ai` AIMenu component does NOT have built-in accept/reject buttons. The `items` callback MUST return accept/reject items for "user-reviewing" status and retry/cancel items for "error" status. Call `ai.acceptChanges()` and `ai.rejectChanges()` from `editor.getExtension(AIExtension)`.
- **Yjs snapshot is binary, not text:** `CollabDocument.yjs_doc` and `CollabVersion.snapshot` store Yjs CRDT binary state, NOT UTF-8 text. Decoding with `.decode("utf-8")` produces garbled output. For diff/summary, use `snapshot_text` (markdown) column instead, populated by frontend's `editor.blocksToMarkdownLossy()`.
- **Dashboard API auth:** Dashboard endpoints use `CurrentUserWithAccess` / `DashboardUser` (requires `system:access` permission). All dashboard routes are under `/api/extensions/dashboard/`.
- **Workflow graph_json is flexible:** DAG node data is stored in `WorkflowDefinition.graph_json` (JSONB). New fields like `required_roles` don't need model migrations — they live inside the JSON. Only top-level fields like `org_bindings` need ALTER TABLE.
- **PhaseConfigPanel is called from WorkflowEditor:** The PhaseConfigPanel is rendered in WorkflowEditor.tsx with `{ data, onUpdate }` props. New props must be optional to avoid breaking the call site.
- **react-day-picker CustomComponents type:** The `Calendar` wrapper from Shadcn/ui uses `react-day-picker` v9 which has `components.DayContent` type issues. Prefer using `modifiers` + `modifiersStyles` for visual customization instead of custom components.
- **Tailwind v4 border FOUC:** In Tailwind CSS v4, the `.border` utility class only sets `border-width` and `border-style` in `@layer utilities`, but `border-color` relies on `@layer base { * { border-color: var(--border) } }`. During Next.js client-side navigation with Turbopack HMR, the base layer can briefly not apply, causing fallback to `currentColor` (black). Fix: always pair `border` with `border-border` to put border-color in the utilities layer (higher cascade priority). Also add `@source inline("border-border")` to globals.css to ensure the utility is generated.
- **DB migration pattern:** New tables/columns use `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS` / `ALTER TABLE ... ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS` in `database.py`'s `migrate_db()`. No Alembic — just raw SQL in the async init function.
- **React derived state pattern:** When a parent manages a list (e.g., React Flow nodes) and a child needs to display/edit a selected item, NEVER store a snapshot of the selected item as independent state. Store only the selected ID and derive the item from the latest array via `useMemo`. Otherwise, edits to the item via state setters won't reflect in the child — it keeps reading the stale snapshot. This caused WorkflowEditor's property panel inputs to be non-functional.
- **Workflow node type consolidation (2026-06-11):** Reduced from 10 to 6 DAG node types. Legacy types are normalised at runtime: phase→subflow, manual_edit→task, sub_workflow→subflow, notify→deprecated (notifications is now an attribute on all nodes). Frontend `DAGNodeType` only has 6 values. Backend uses `_normalise_node_type()` in both `local_executor.py` and `temporal/workflows.py` for backward compat. The `migration.ts` template still handles all legacy types for v1→v2 migration.
- **Subflow double-click navigation:** `useWorkflowDAG` already has `enterSubflow`/`exitSubflow` for sub-graph editing. WorkflowEditor tracks `activeSubflowId` state, renders a breadcrumb bar when inside a subflow, and handles `onNodeDoubleClick` for subflow nodes. The `SubflowConfigPanel` is functionally identical to the old `PhaseConfigPanel` (roles, org binding, AI assist, notifications).
- **Template publish/unpublish semantics:** `is_template` means "this IS a template" (structural flag). `template_status` means the publication state (draft/published/pending_approval/rejected). When unpublishing, only change `template_status` back to "draft" — do NOT set `is_template=false`. The template list filters by `is_template=true`, so clearing it makes the template vanish from the list even though graph data is intact in the DB.
- **Permission gate `project:create`:** Removed from default "user" role in `_ROLE_DEFAULTS`. The `_ensure_role` function auto-resets DB roles that have drifted (extra permissions beyond coded defaults). Regular users need explicit permission or admin role to create projects.
- **`require_super_admin` dependency:** Added to auth middleware for operations requiring system-level admin (workflow definition edit/delete/publish). Checks `role.is_system` or wildcard `"*"` in permissions.
- **Phase-scoped chapter access:** `ProjectChapter.phase_node` column (VARCHAR(100), nullable) tracks which workflow phase a chapter belongs to. Set during `init_phase` activity using `chapter_range` from workflow graph. Non-owner/manager users can only edit chapters in the current active phase.
- **Activity log pattern:** `ActivityLog` model tracks project actions (who/what/when). Use `log_activity()` helper from routers — pass db session, project_id, user_id, action string. Avoids N+1 queries by keeping the helper lightweight (just `db.add`).
- **Batch query pattern for list endpoints:** Avoid per-item queries in list endpoints. Use `group_by` queries to batch-fetch counts (chapters, members, completed chapters) in one SQL statement per metric, then join in Python.
- **Custom roles need system:access:** All extension API endpoints (projects, workflow, dashboard, etc.) require `system:access` permission. When creating custom roles, always include `system:access` + `kb:read` + `doc:read` as base permissions. The default "user" role has these, but custom roles do not inherit them automatically.
- **Project chapters have no REST creation endpoint:** `ProjectChapter` rows are created via `_import_template_outline()` (from ExtractionTemplate) or `copy-from` endpoint, not via individual POST. To create chapters for testing, insert directly into DB with `created_at`, `updated_at`, `word_count_current` fields.
- **Departments API returns nested tree:** `GET /api/extensions/departments` returns `{ departments: [rootNode] }` where children are nested inside `rootNode.children[]`. Must flatten the tree to find specific departments by name.
- **RAGFlow parser file type support varies by chunk_method:** The `manual` (report) parser only supports PDF and DOCX. The `naive` parser supports many formats. Always validate file types against `_PARSER_SUPPORTED_EXTENSIONS` before uploading to RAGFlow — otherwise files upload OK but fail during chunking with a cryptic RAGFlow error. The mapping is in `DocumentService._PARSER_SUPPORTED_EXTENSIONS` (service.py) and mirrored as `CHUNK_METHOD_ACCEPT` in the frontend `knowledge/page.tsx`.
- **Approval endpoint is `/submit-approval`** (not `/approval/submit`): The project approval submission uses `POST /projects/{id}/submit-approval` with `ApprovalSubmitRequest` schema requiring `steps[].step_order` and `steps[].reviewer_id` (UUID).
- **Output API uses FormData, not JSON:** `POST /api/extensions/output/generate` expects `source`, `format`, `layout_template_id` as Form fields, plus optional `file` upload OR `content` string for markdown source. Not a JSON body.
- **DB migration pattern for new columns:** When adding a column to an existing table's SQLAlchemy model, you MUST also add it to the raw SQL CREATE TABLE in `database.py` AND add an `ALTER TABLE ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS` for existing databases. Just updating the model is not enough — the raw SQL runs first on fresh DBs.
- **Comment system is fully functional:** `CollabComment` model + `CommentService` CRUD + threaded replies (parent_id) + resolve/reopen. API: `/docmgr/documents/{id}/comments` (GET/POST), `/docmgr/comments/{id}` (PUT/DELETE/resolve/reopen).
- **Version system is fully functional:** `CollabVersion` + `VersionService` with create/list/diff(difflib.SequenceMatcher)/restore. API: `/docmgr/documents/{id}/versions` (GET/POST), diff (GET ?from=&to=), restore (POST).
- **Reminder service triggers via endpoint:** `POST /api/extensions/dashboard/check-reminders` scans `ProjectTimeline` entries and milestones, creates `Notification` rows with urgency classification (overdue/urgent/warning/notice) and dedup.
- **CSRF token for browser console API testing:** Get from cookie `csrf_token`, pass as `X-CSRF-Token` header. Cookie name is literally `csrf_token` (not `__Host-csrf`).
- **Template list endpoint:** `GET /api/extensions/output/templates` returns `{items: [...], total: N}` (not `/layout-templates`).
- **Temporal sandbox incompatibility:** Temporal's default sandboxed workflow runner cannot import FastAPI (sniffio._ThreadLocal restriction). Must use `UnsandboxedWorkflowRunner()` from `temporalio.worker` — import is `from temporalio.worker import UnsandboxedWorkflowRunner`, NOT from `temporalio.worker.workflow_sandbox`.
- **Temporal in Docker needs TEMPORAL_URL:** Gateway runs in its own container, so `localhost:7233` points to the gateway itself. Must set `TEMPORAL_URL=eai-flow-temporal:7233` in docker-compose-dev.yaml. The temporal server compose file is `docker/docker-compose.temporal.yaml` and must be combined with `docker-compose.extensions.yaml` (which defines postgres-ext).
- **Temporal requires PostgreSQL user:** Temporal auto-setup needs a `temporal` PostgreSQL user with SUPERUSER and a `temporal` database. Must create before starting the server.
- **docker compose restart vs up:** `docker compose restart` does NOT re-read compose file changes (new env vars, etc). Must use `docker compose up -d <service>` to recreate the container with updated config.
- **DB session scoping in FastAPI endpoints:** When using `Depends(get_db)` in FastAPI, the DB session is tied to the request lifecycle. If you open a separate `get_db_context()` inside the endpoint, the `Depends(get_db)` session may be closed by middleware. For endpoints that need full control, remove `db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)` and use `async with get_db_context() as db:` inside the function body instead.
- **Workflow-status API includes graph_json:** The `GET /workflow/projects/{id}/workflow-status` endpoint returns `graph_json` (the full DAG structure) along with node statuses. Frontend should use this to render the workflow progress view, avoiding a separate API call to `/workflow/definitions/{id}` (which may have different permission requirements).
- **Permission gap for workflow definition read:** `WorkflowReader` requires `project:read` permission, but the 项目经理 role doesn't have it. The `workflow-status` endpoint was changed to use `CurrentUserWithAccess` (requires `system:access`) instead.
- **Cross-layer callback pattern (harness↔app):** When harness code (e.g. `present_files` tool) needs to trigger app-layer logic (e.g. docmgr sync), use the callback registry at `deerflow.tools.callbacks`. App layer registers callbacks at gateway startup via `register_present_files_callback()`. Harness fires them via `fire_present_files_callbacks()`. The sync tool is synchronous but the callbacks are async — use `loop.create_task()` to schedule background tasks. This avoids violating the harness→app import boundary.
- **Background DB sessions outside HTTP requests:** Code running outside the HTTP request lifecycle (tool callbacks, background tasks) must use `get_db_context()` from `app.extensions.database` to get an independent DB session. Never use `Depends(get_db)` outside FastAPI endpoints.
- **File upload pattern in React:** NEVER use `<label>` wrapping `<input type="file">` — causes double-click issue on some browsers. Also NEVER use `display:none` / `className="hidden"` on file inputs — prevents the native file dialog from opening even when `.click()` is called from a user gesture. Instead: use `<div>` with explicit `onClick={() => ref.current?.click()}` and hide the input with `className="sr-only"` (keeps it renderable but invisible: `display:block; position:absolute; width:1px; height:1px; overflow:hidden`).
- **Optimistic lock pattern for review actions:** Use conditional UPDATE (`WHERE status='pending'`) instead of read-check-then-write. The read-then-write pattern allows race conditions under concurrent requests. Check `result.rowcount == 0` to detect conflicts.
- **Phase-scoped chapter rollback:** When a review rejection rolls back to a writing phase, must also reset `ProjectChapter.status` from "completed"/"approved" back to "pending" for chapters in that phase scope, not just the project's `current_phase_node`.
- **SLOT_PERMISSIONS must be registered in PROJECT_PERMISSIONS:** Any permission string used in SLOT_PERMISSIONS (like "chapter:confirm", "report:submit") must also exist in the PROJECT_PERMISSIONS list, otherwise the permission system can't validate or grant them properly.
- **Dead code after return in Python:** When a function has an early return (e.g., inside an if block) followed by code at the same indentation level, the subsequent code is unreachable dead code. Python doesn't warn about this — must be caught by code review.
- **Corrupted `.next/dev` cache after Docker Desktop crash (2026-06-16):** When Docker Desktop's Linux engine crashes/restarts (unclean shutdown), the Turbopack `.next/dev` cache (can balloon to ~4.4GB) gets corrupted. Symptom: `next dev --turbo` shows `✓ Ready` but `○ Compiling <route> ...` NEVER reaches `✓ Compiled`; ALL routes hang (nginx returns `502 upstream prematurely closed connection`); CPU stays LOW (~4%, blocked — not a spin). `docker compose restart frontend` does NOT fix it because `.next` lives in the container's writable layer and the SAME container is restarted (cache persists). **Fix:** force-recreate the container for a fresh writable layer: `cd docker && docker compose -p eai-docker -f docker-compose-dev.yaml -f docker-compose.extensions.yaml -f docker-compose.temporal.yaml -f docker-compose.ragflow.yaml up -d --force-recreate frontend`. After this, cold compiles run hot (CPU >200%) and complete in 45–99s; routes return 200. Distinguish from a real code hang: a genuine compile spins CPU high; a corrupted-cache hang sits at ~4% CPU with no `✓ Compiled` ever. See [[bug-087]] in buglog. The dev compose stack uses ALL FOUR files (dev+extensions+temporal+ragflow) from working dir `docker/` — get the exact list from the container label `com.docker.compose.project.config_files`. NOTE: `docker compose -p <proj> ps/logs` work WITHOUT `-f` (filter by project label), but `up/restart/force-recreate` REQUIRE `-f` (no compose file at repo root).

- **MCP is the ONLY bridge from the AI agent to app-layer extensions (2026-06-18):** The harness (`deerflow.*`, where the agent runs) is forbidden from `import app.*` (enforced by `tests/test_harness_boundary.py`). An extension that wants the agent to use its data/tools MUST expose a stdio **MCP server** (`<ext>/mcp.py`, mirroring `app/extensions/project/mcp.py`) and register it in `extensions_config.json` → `mcpServers` (enabled). The harness's `langchain-mcp-adapters` loader turns its `Tool`s into agent tools. MCP handlers **lazily** `import app.*` inside the function (the MCP server is a separate process, so the boundary doesn't apply to it). DB URL comes from `get_extensions_config().database.url` inside the handler — **NEVER** `PROJECT_DB_URL`. `app/extensions/data_source/mcp.py` now follows this pattern (4 read-only tools: list_data_sources, get_data_source_schema, query_data_source, test_data_source).
- **Two PostgreSQL databases — don't confuse (2026-06-18):** (1) **extensions DB** = `agentflow`, resolved via `get_extensions_config().database.url` / `EXTENSIONS_DB_*` env, container `eai-flow-postgres-ext` — this is where `users`, `roles`, `data_sources`, docmgr, project tables all live (auto-created at startup by `database.py::init_db` → `Base.metadata.create_all`). (2) **project-db** = `PROJECT_DB_URL` → `project-db:5432/project`, a *separate* DB used only by the project MCP server. New extension tables go in the extensions DB; MCP servers must connect there.
- **Two separate auth systems (2026-06-18):** gateway auth (`app.gateway.auth`, `AuthContext` + `thread_store`, guards `/api/langgraph/*` runs) is DIFFERENT from extensions auth (`app.extensions.auth.middleware.get_current_user` → `CurrentUser` from `app.extensions.schemas`, guards `/api/extensions/*`). Extension routers use the latter: `current_user: CurrentUser = Depends(get_current_user)` + `db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)`. The `tests/_router_auth_helpers.py` stub is for the GATEWAY auth system, not extensions — for extension router tests use FastAPI `dependency_overrides` on `get_current_user`/`get_db`.
- **SQLAlchemy 2.0 `mapped_column(default=...)` only fires at flush, not construction (2026-06-18):** Accessing a defaulted attribute on a non-persisted instance returns `None`. If code/tests need defaults at construction time, add `def __init__(self, **kwargs): kwargs.setdefault(...); super().__init__(**kwargs)` (the `DataSource` model does this). Explicit kwargs always win.
- **报告平台 data_source / plugin modules were frontend-only stubs (2026-06-18):** Both tabs in 设置 were frontend-complete but backend-empty. `data_source` now has a full backend (PR #4: model + CRUD + test/sync + read-only MCP). `plugin` (`/plugins/registry|instances|api-keys`) backend STILL does not exist — every frontend call 404s. **【已作废 2026-08-03】plugin 全栈已删除**（见 docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-03-role-permissions-tab-ui-design.md）：模型/路由/前端/DB 表全删，从未启用。不要再"建 plugin 后端"。MCP 接线的需求现由 `app_center` + 手动 extensions_config 注册承载（app_center = 导航外壳，非能力包，详见 [[dcs-qa-cement-energy-rca]] 核实）。
- **STARTUP SEEDING IS BROKEN by a pre-existing migrate_db bug (2026-06-18):** In `gateway/app.py` lifespan, `init_db → migrate_db → seed_db` shared ONE try/except, so when `migrate_db()` threw (`role_permissions` table lacks the `UNIQUE(role,permission)` constraint its `ON CONFLICT (role,permission) DO NOTHING` needs — `_seed_role_permissions` in `database.py`), it aborted `seed_db()` at EVERY startup → NO seed data (incl. any new plugin/template seeds) ever auto-ran on restart. Symptom: tables created by `init_db` exist, but seed tables stay empty. Fix applied: isolate `seed_db` in its own try/except so it runs regardless of `migrate_db` failure. The root `migrate_db` role_permissions bug is STILL unfixed (out of scope) — flag for cleanup (add `UNIQUE(role,permission)` to the `role_permissions` CREATE or fix the ON CONFLICT). Diagnose by running `init_db/migrate_db/seed_db` manually in the container. Also: `docker compose logs gateway` shows NO stdout (logging goes elsewhere) — don't rely on it; query the DB directly to verify seeding.
- **role_permissions schema-drift FIXED (2026-06-18, commit f4d6516c):** The `migrate_db` startup throw above is now fixed. Root cause was an *existing* `role_permissions` table created from an older `CREATE TABLE` (missing `id DEFAULT gen_random_uuid()`, `created_at DEFAULT now()`, and the `UNIQUE(role,permission)` constraint) — `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS` won't upgrade an existing table, so `_seed_role_permissions`' `INSERT ... ON CONFLICT (role,permission)` failed (first `InvalidColumnReferenceError`, then `NotNullViolationError` on id, then on created_at). Fix = idempotent `ALTER COLUMN ... SET DEFAULT` for id + created_at + `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS` on (role,permission), all in `migrate_db`. Verified: empty role_permissions → restart → re-seeds 27 rows through the original `init_db→migrate_db→seed_db` path. Startup seeding (plugins, templates, role_permissions) now runs normally.
- **IM channel construction signature (2026-06-18):** Real channel classes (`FeishuChannel`, `WechatChannel`, etc.) define `__init__(self, bus, config)` — they do NOT take a `name` arg; they hard-code it (`super().__init__(name="feishu", bus=bus, config=config)`). `ChannelService._start_channel` instantiates them as `channel_cls(bus=self.bus, config=config)`. When writing channel unit tests with a fake channel, mirror this signature (no `name` param) or `_start_channel` raises `TypeError: __init__() missing 1 required positional argument: 'name'`. The base `Channel.__init__` DOES take `name`, but every concrete subclass overrides `__init__` to drop it.
- **Dev `app/channels/manager.py` is heavily customized and had a latent import bug (2026-06-18):** It diverges from upstream `bytedance/main` (different DEFAULT URLs — dev uses port 4024/4001 vs upstream 8001; `_get_client` calls `get_client(url=...)` with NO auth headers by dev design). It used `create_internal_auth_headers()` in `_fetch_gateway` but the import (`from app.gateway.internal_auth import create_internal_auth_headers`) was missing → ruff F821 + silent NameError caught by a bare except → `/models` & `/memory` channel commands returned "Failed to fetch". The helper exists in `app/gateway/internal_auth.py`; only the import line was dropped. The `_get_client`-no-headers behavior is intentional (don't "fix" it — test `test_get_client_includes_csrf_header_and_cookie` expects upstream behavior and is a known pre-existing failure). See [[bug-139]].

- **mcp 1.28 FastMCP launch API (2026-06-19, [[bug-cad-001]]):** `host`/`port`/`streamable_http_path` are `FastMCP()` CONSTRUCTOR kwargs — `mcp.run()` only accepts `transport` (+ `mount_path`). The constructor default `host='127.0.0.1'` MUST be overridden to `0.0.0.0` or the server is unreachable from other containers over the docker network. Office-Word-MCP-Server uses an OLDER mcp SDK and passes them to `run()` — do NOT copy that launch pattern verbatim into a new server. Inspect the installed API first: `python -c "import inspect; from mcp.server.fastmcp import FastMCP; print(inspect.signature(FastMCP.run)); print(inspect.signature(FastMCP.__init__))"`.
- **Dev compose = docker-compose-dev.yaml ONLY (2026-06-19):** `scripts/docker.sh:16` runs `docker compose -p eai-docker -f docker-compose-dev.yaml` — it does NOT auto-include the extensions/temporal/ragflow overlays.
